+import SwiftUI
+import UIKit
+import React
+
+// The phone's app UI, in SwiftUI so it is the system's own iOS 27 design (Liquid Glass bars, toolbars,
+// sheets, lists and buttons). Every network, photo, capture and 3D call goes through the native modules
+// the React Native app already uses and tests (ObjectsBridge, NativeCaptureCoordinator, CompanionPairing),
+// so pairing, pinning and uploads behave exactly as before. Pairing itself stays a React Native screen.
+
+// MARK: - The native modules, called directly
+
+@objc private protocol ObjectsBridgeCalls {
+    @objc(callOptions:resolve:reject:) func call(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(sendCaptureOptions:resolve:reject:) func sendCapture(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(choosePhotosOptions:resolve:reject:) func choosePhotos(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(sendChosenOptions:resolve:reject:) func sendChosen(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(pickPhotosOptions:resolve:reject:) func pickPhotos(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(takePhotoOptions:resolve:reject:) func takePhoto(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(downloadOptions:resolve:reject:) func download(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(showModelOptions:resolve:reject:) func showModel(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+}
+@objc private protocol CaptureCalls {
+    @objc(status:reject:) func status(_ resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(drafts:reject:) func drafts(_ resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(start:resolve:reject:) func start(_ o: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+}
+@objc private protocol PairingCalls {
+    @objc(statusResolve:reject:) func status(_ resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+    @objc(checkResolve:reject:) func check(_ resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock)
+}
+
+/// A refusal in one sentence the screen can show as it is.
+struct MacError: Error, Equatable {
+    var code: String
+    var message: String
+    var needsPairing: Bool { ["pairing-required", "pairing-changed", "certificate-changed", "scope-denied", "pairing"].contains(code) }
+    static let unreachable = MacError(code: "unreachable", message: "Your Mac isn't answering. Check it's on and awake, then try again.")
+}
+
+private func native(_ name: String) -> NSObject? { (NSClassFromString(name) as? NSObject.Type)?.init() }
+
+@MainActor private func promise(_ body: (@escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock, @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock) -> Void) async throws -> Any? {
+    try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { (continuation: CheckedContinuation<Any?, Error>) in
+        var done = false
+        body({ value in
+            guard !done else { return }; done = true
+            continuation.resume(returning: value)
+        }, { code, message, _ in
+            guard !done else { return }; done = true
+            continuation.resume(throwing: MacError(code: code ?? "failed", message: message ?? "Something went wrong talking to your Mac. Try again."))
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+/// The Mac, through the paired, pinned connection the native modules keep.
+@MainActor final class MacConnection {
+    private let bridge = native("ObjectsBridge").map { unsafeBitCast($0, to: ObjectsBridgeCalls.self) }
+    private let captureModule = native("NativeCaptureCoordinator").map { unsafeBitCast($0, to: CaptureCalls.self) }
+    private let pairingModule = native("CompanionPairing").map { unsafeBitCast($0, to: PairingCalls.self) }
+
+    private func require<T>(_ value: T?) throws -> T {
+        guard let value else { throw MacError(code: "unavailable", message: "This needs the iPhone app.") }
+        return value
+    }
+
+    private func request<T: Decodable>(_ method: String, _ path: String, _ json: [String: Any]? = nil, as type: T.Type) async throws -> T {
+        let bridge = try require(bridge)
+        var options: [String: Any] = ["method": method, "path": path]
+        if let json { options["json"] = json }
+        let reply = try await promise { bridge.call(options as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) } as? [String: Any]
+        let status = reply?["status"] as? Int ?? 0
+        let data = Data(((reply?["body"] as? String) ?? "").utf8)
+        if (200..<300).contains(status) {
+            do { return try JSONDecoder().decode(T.self, from: data) }
+            catch { throw MacError(code: "invalid-response", message: "Your Mac sent something this app doesn't understand. Update both apps.") }
+        }
+        let error = (try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any])?["error"] as? [String: Any]
+        let message = error?["message"] as? String
+        switch status {
+        case 401, 403: throw MacError(code: "pairing", message: "Connect to your Mac again.")
+        case 404 where path == "/v1/objects": throw MacError(code: "not-set-up", message: "Your Mac isn't ready to make things yet. Open Scan to Make on your Mac.")
+        case 404: throw MacError(code: "not-found", message: "This is no longer on your Mac.")
+        case 409: throw MacError(code: "conflict", message: message ?? "Wait until the current step finishes.")
+        default: throw MacError(code: "failed", message: "Something went wrong on your Mac. Try again.")
+        }
+    }
+    private struct ObjectReply: Decodable { var object: StmObject }
+    private struct ListReply: Decodable { var objects: [StmObject] }
+    private struct Deleted: Decodable { var deleted: Bool }
+
+    func list() async throws -> [StmObject] { try await request("GET", "/v1/objects", as: ListReply.self).objects }
+    func get(_ id: String) async throws -> StmObject { try await request("GET", "/v1/objects/\(id)", as: ObjectReply.self).object }
+    func create(_ instructions: String) async throws -> StmObject {
+        let text = instructions.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+        return try await request("POST", "/v1/objects", text.isEmpty ? [:] : ["instructions": text], as: ObjectReply.self).object
+    }
+    func start(_ id: String) async throws -> StmObject { try await request("POST", "/v1/objects/\(id)/start", as: ObjectReply.self).object }
+    func correct(_ id: String, _ note: String) async throws -> StmObject { try await request("POST", "/v1/objects/\(id)/corrections", ["note": note], as: ObjectReply.self).object }
+    func closeup(_ id: String, bestGuess: Bool) async throws -> StmObject { try await request("POST", "/v1/objects/\(id)/closeup", ["bestGuess": bestGuess], as: ObjectReply.self).object }
+    func print(_ id: String, checked: Bool) async throws -> StmObject { try await request("POST", "/v1/objects/\(id)/print", checked ? ["checked": true] : nil, as: ObjectReply.self).object }
+    func plateClear(_ id: String) async throws -> StmObject { try await request("POST", "/v1/objects/\(id)/plate", ["clear": true], as: ObjectReply.self).object }
+    func delete(_ id: String) async throws { _ = try await request("DELETE", "/v1/objects/\(id)", as: Deleted.self) }
+
+    /// Photos picked for a new object; they stay on this phone until sent.
+    func choosePhotos(limit: Int) async throws -> [ChosenPhoto] {
+        let bridge = try require(bridge)
+        let chosen = try await promise { bridge.choosePhotos(["limit": limit] as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) } as? [[String: Any]] ?? []
+        return chosen.compactMap { item in
+            guard let id = item["id"] as? String, let uri = item["uri"] as? String, let url = URL(string: uri) else { return nil }
+            return ChosenPhoto(id: id, url: url)
+        }
+    }
+    func sendChosen(_ id: String, _ photos: [ChosenPhoto]) async throws {
+        guard !photos.isEmpty else { return }
+        let bridge = try require(bridge)
+        _ = try await promise { bridge.sendChosen(["objectId": id, "ids": photos.map(\.id)] as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) }
+    }
+    /// Close-ups for an object that asked for them; sent as soon as they're picked or taken.
+    func addCloseups(_ id: String, camera: Bool) async throws -> Int {
+        let bridge = try require(bridge)
+        let reply = try await promise {
+            if camera { bridge.takePhoto(["objectId": id] as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) }
+            else { bridge.pickPhotos(["objectId": id, "limit": 12] as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) }
+        } as? [String: Any]
+        return reply?["photosSent"] as? Int ?? 0
+    }
+    func download(_ object: StmObject, _ name: String) async throws -> URL? {
+        let bridge = try require(bridge)
+        let reply = try await promise {
+            bridge.download(["objectId": object.id, "name": name, "version": object.updatedAtMs] as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1)
+        } as? [String: Any]
+        return (reply?["uri"] as? String).flatMap(URL.init(string:))
+    }
+    func showModel(_ object: StmObject) async throws {
+        let bridge = try require(bridge)
+        _ = try await promise { bridge.showModel(["objectId": object.id, "version": object.updatedAtMs] as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) }
+    }
+
+    // LiDAR capture: the native Object Capture flow, with the object's id as its origin, so a finished
+    // scan that wasn't sent is found again and sent.
+    func canScan() async -> Bool {
+        guard let captureModule else { return false }
+        let status = (try? await promise { captureModule.status($0, reject: $1) }) as? [String: Any]
+        return status?["supported"] as? Bool == true && status?["lidar"] as? Bool != false
+    }
+    func scan(_ id: String) async throws -> String? {
+        let captureModule = try require(captureModule)
+        let origin: [String: Any] = ["projectId": id, "revision": 0, "snapshot": "{}", "context": NSNull()]
+        let draft = try await promise { captureModule.start(origin as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) } as? [String: Any]
+        return draft?["status"] as? String == "ready" ? draft?["runID"] as? String : nil
+    }
+    func finishedScan(_ id: String) async -> String? {
+        guard let captureModule else { return nil }
+        let drafts = (try? await promise { captureModule.drafts($0, reject: $1) }) as? [[String: Any]] ?? []
+        let draft = drafts.first { ($0["origin"] as? [String: Any])?["projectId"] as? String == id }
+        return draft?["status"] as? String == "ready" ? draft?["runID"] as? String : nil
+    }
+    /// Sends the scan's model and up to 24 of its own photos (only what the Mac lacks), then starts.
+    func sendScan(_ id: String, run: String) async throws -> StmObject {
+        let bridge = try require(bridge)
+        _ = try await promise { bridge.sendCapture(["objectId": id, "runID": run, "maxPhotos": 24] as NSDictionary, resolve: $0, reject: $1) }
+        return try await start(id)
+    }
+
+    struct Pairing: Equatable { var paired: Bool; var address: String? }
+    func pairing(check: Bool) async -> Pairing {
+        guard let pairingModule else { return Pairing(paired: false, address: nil) }
+        let reply = (try? await promise { check ? pairingModule.check($0, reject: $1) : pairingModule.status($0, reject: $1) }) as? [String: Any]
+        let address = (reply?["baseUrl"] as? String).flatMap(URL.init(string:))?.host
+        return Pairing(paired: reply?["state"] as? String == "paired", address: address)
+    }
+}
+
+struct ChosenPhoto: Identifiable, Equatable { var id: String; var url: URL }
+
+// MARK: - State
+
+@MainActor @Observable final class ObjectsStore {
+    let mac = MacConnection()
+    var objects: [StmObject] = []
+    var loaded = false
+    var error: MacError?
+    var connection: MacConnection.Pairing?
+    var canScan = false
+    var onPair: () -> Void = {}
+
+    func load() async {
+        do { objects = try await mac.list(); error = nil }
+        catch let failure as MacError { error = failure }
+        catch { self.error = .unreachable }
+        loaded = true
+    }
+    func replace(_ object: StmObject) {
+        if let index = objects.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == object.id }) { objects[index] = object } else { objects.insert(object, at: 0) }
+    }
+    func remove(_ id: String) { objects.removeAll { $0.id == id } }
+    var anyBusy: Bool { objects.contains { ObjectWords($0).busy } }
+    func refreshConnection(check: Bool = false) async { connection = await mac.pairing(check: check) }
+}
+
+private let cobalt = Color(uiColor: UIColor { $0.userInterfaceStyle == .dark
+    ? UIColor(red: 0x7B / 255, green: 0x95 / 255, blue: 0xFF / 255, alpha: 1)
+    : UIColor(red: 0x24 / 255, green: 0x48 / 255, blue: 0xD8 / 255, alpha: 1) })
+
+private extension ObjectWords.Tone {
+    var color: Color {
+        switch self {
+        case .pass: .green
+        case .fail: .red
+        case .accent: cobalt
+        case .pending: .orange
+        case .neutral: .secondary
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// MARK: - Root: the list
+
+struct ObjectsRootView: View {
+    @State var store: ObjectsStore
+    let pairingKey: Int
+    @State private var path: [String] = []
+    @State private var creating = false
+    @State private var settings = false
+    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase
+
+    var body: some View {
+        NavigationStack(path: $path) {
+            ObjectsListView(store: store, startNew: { creating = true })
+                .navigationTitle("Scan to Make")
+                .toolbar {
+                    ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarLeading) {
+                        Button { settings = true } label: { Label("Settings", systemImage: "gearshape") }
+                    }
+                    ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
+                        Button { creating = true } label: { Label("New", systemImage: "plus") }
+                            .buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
+                    }
+                }
+                .navigationDestination(for: String.self) { id in
+                    ObjectDetailView(store: store, id: id, close: { path.removeAll() })
+                }
+        }
+        .tint(cobalt)
+        .sheet(isPresented: $creating) {
+            NewObjectView(store: store) { object in
+                creating = false
+                store.replace(object)
+                path = [object.id]
+            }
+        }
+        .sheet(isPresented: $settings) { SettingsView(store: store) }
+        .task(id: pairingKey) {
+            async let scan = store.mac.canScan()
+            await store.refreshConnection()
+            await store.load()
+            store.canScan = await scan
+        }
+        // While something is touching up or printing, keep the list current.
+        .task(id: store.anyBusy && scenePhase == .active) {
+            guard store.anyBusy, scenePhase == .active else { return }
+            while !Task.isCancelled {
+                try? await Task.sleep(for: .seconds(3))
+                await store.load()
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct ObjectsListView: View {
+    @Bindable var store: ObjectsStore
+    let startNew: () -> Void
+    @State private var deleting: StmObject?
+
+    var body: some View {
+        List {
+            Section {
+                ConnectionRow(connection: store.connection, error: store.error) { store.onPair() }
+            }
+            if store.objects.isEmpty, store.loaded, store.error == nil {
+                Section {
+                    ContentUnavailableView {
+                        Label("Nothing yet", systemImage: "cube")
+                    } description: {
+                        Text("Describe something to make, or scan a real object to copy it.")
+                    } actions: {
+                        Button("Make Something", action: startNew).buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
+                    }
+                }
+            } else if !store.objects.isEmpty {
+                Section("Your Things") {
+                    ForEach(store.objects) { object in
+                        NavigationLink(value: object.id) { ObjectRow(store: store, object: object) }
+                            .swipeActions {
+                                Button("Delete", systemImage: "trash", role: .destructive) { deleting = object }
+                            }
+                            .contextMenu {
+                                Button("Delete", systemImage: "trash", role: .destructive) { deleting = object }
+                            }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        .refreshable { await store.load() }
+        .confirmationDialog("Delete this from your Mac?", isPresented: Binding(get: { deleting != nil }, set: { if !$0 { deleting = nil } }),
+                            titleVisibility: .visible, presenting: deleting) { object in
+            Button("Delete", role: .destructive) {
+                Task {
+                    do { try await store.mac.delete(object.id); store.remove(object.id) }
+                    catch let failure as MacError { store.error = failure }
+                }
+            }
+        } message: { _ in Text("Its model, photos and renders are removed from your Mac.") }
+    }
+}

+/// The paired Mac and whether it answers: the first thing to check when something doesn't arrive.
+struct ConnectionRow: View {
+    let connection: MacConnection.Pairing?
+    let error: MacError?
+    let pair: () -> Void
+    var body: some View {
+        if let error, error.needsPairing || error.code == "unreachable" || error.code == "not-set-up" {
+            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
+                Label(error.message, systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill").foregroundStyle(.orange)
+                if error.needsPairing { Button("Connect to Your Mac", action: pair).buttonStyle(.glass) }
+            }
+        } else if connection?.paired == true {
+            Label { Text("Connected to your Mac") + Text(connection?.address.map { "  \($0)" } ?? "").foregroundStyle(.secondary) }
+                icon: { Image(systemName: "desktopcomputer").foregroundStyle(.green) }
+        } else if connection != nil {
+            HStack {
+                Label("Not connected to a Mac", systemImage: "desktopcomputer.trianglebadge.exclamationmark").foregroundStyle(.orange)
+                Spacer()
+                Button("Connect", action: pair).buttonStyle(.glass)
+            }
+        } else {
+            Label("Checking your Mac…", systemImage: "desktopcomputer").foregroundStyle(.secondary)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct ObjectRow: View {
+    let store: ObjectsStore
+    let object: StmObject
+    var body: some View {
+        let words = ObjectWords(object)
+        HStack(spacing: 14) {
+            RenderImage(store: store, object: object, render: object.current?.renders.first)
+                .frame(width: 60, height: 60)
+                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
+            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 3) {
+                Text(words.title).font(.headline).lineLimit(2)
+                HStack(spacing: 6) {
+                    if words.busy { ProgressView().controlSize(.mini) }
+                    Text(words.status.components(separatedBy: ". ").first ?? words.status)
+                        .font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(words.tone.color).lineLimit(2)
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        .padding(.vertical, 2)
+    }
+}
+
+/// A render downloaded once per object version, on the build-plate colour while it loads.
+struct RenderImage: View {
+    let store: ObjectsStore
+    let object: StmObject
+    let render: String?
+    @State private var image: UIImage?
+    var body: some View {
+        ZStack {
+            Color(white: 0.17)
+            if let image { Image(uiImage: image).resizable().scaledToFit() }
+            else if render == nil { Image(systemName: "cube").font(.title2).foregroundStyle(.white.opacity(0.5)) }
+        }
+        .task(id: "\(object.id)#\(object.updatedAtMs)#\(render ?? "")") {
+            guard let render, let name = render.split(separator: "/").last else { image = nil; return }
+            if let url = try? await store.mac.download(object, "renders/\(name)"), let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url) { image = UIImage(data: data) }
+        }
+    }
+}

+// MARK: - New
+
+struct NewObjectView: View {
+    let store: ObjectsStore
+    let started: (StmObject) -> Void
+    @State private var instructions = ""
+    @State private var photos: [ChosenPhoto] = []
+    @State private var working: String?
+    @State private var problem: String?
+    @State private var askedForDescription = false
+    @FocusState private var describing: Bool
+    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
+    private var described: Bool { !instructions.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty }
+
+    var body: some View {
+        NavigationStack {
+            Form {
+                Section {
+                    TextField("A clip that holds a 6 mm cable to the edge of my desk", text: $instructions, axis: .vertical)
+                        .lineLimit(3...10)
+                        .focused($describing)
+                } header: {
+                    Text("Describe It")
+                } footer: {
+                    Text(askedForDescription && !described
+                         ? "Describe what you want made first. Photos only show what it goes with."
+                         : "What it is, what it fits or attaches to, and any sizes. Product names, model numbers and links all help.")
+                        .foregroundStyle(askedForDescription && !described ? .red : .secondary)
+                }
+                Section {
+                    if !photos.isEmpty {
+                        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
+                            HStack(spacing: 10) {
+                                ForEach(photos) { photo in
+                                    ChosenThumbnail(photo: photo) { photos.removeAll { $0.id == photo.id } }
+                                }
+                            }
+                            .padding(.vertical, 6)
+                        }
+                    }
+                    Button { Task { await addPhotos() } } label: {
+                        Label(photos.isEmpty ? "Add Photos" : "Add More Photos", systemImage: "photo.badge.plus")
+                    }
+                    .disabled(working != nil || photos.count >= 12)
+                } header: {
+                    Text("Photos")
+                } footer: {
+                    Text("Optional. Show the thing it goes with, from a couple of angles.")
+                }
+                if !store.canScan {
+                    Section { } footer: { Text("Scanning needs an iPhone or iPad with LiDAR.") }
+                }
+                if let problem {
+                    Section { Label(problem, systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill").foregroundStyle(.red) }
+                }
+            }
+            .navigationTitle("Make Something")
+            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
+            .scrollDismissesKeyboard(.interactively)
+            // The actions stay in view above the form, and above the keyboard while typing.
+            .safeAreaBar(edge: .bottom) {
+                VStack(spacing: 10) {
+                    Button { Task { await make() } } label: {
+                        Label(working == "make" ? "Sending to Your Mac…" : "Make It", systemImage: "wand.and.sparkles")
+                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
+                    }
+                    .buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
+                    .controlSize(.extraLarge)
+                    .disabled(working != nil)
+                    .accessibilityLabel(working == "make" ? "Sending to your Mac" : "Make It")
+                    if store.canScan && !describing {
+                        Button { Task { await scan() } } label: {
+                            Label(working == "scan" ? "Sending to Your Mac…" : "Scan It Instead", systemImage: "viewfinder")
+                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
+                        }
+                        .buttonStyle(.glass)
+                        .controlSize(.extraLarge)
+                        .disabled(working != nil)
+                        .accessibilityLabel(working == "scan" ? "Sending to your Mac" : "Scan It Instead")
+                    }
+                }
+                .padding(.horizontal, 20)
+                .padding(.vertical, 10)
+            }
+            .toolbar {
+                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) { Button("Cancel") { dismiss() }.disabled(working != nil) }
+                if describing {
+                    ToolbarItem(placement: .confirmationAction) { Button("Done") { describing = false } }
+                }
+            }
+            .interactiveDismissDisabled(working != nil)
+        }
+        .tint(cobalt)
+        .onAppear { describing = true }
+    }
+
+    private func run(_ kind: String, _ body: () async throws -> Void) async {
+        guard working == nil else { return }
+        working = kind; problem = nil
+        defer { working = nil }
+        do { try await body() }
+        catch let failure as MacError {
+            if failure.code == "cancelled" { return }
+            if failure.needsPairing { dismiss(); store.onPair(); return }
+            problem = failure.message
+        } catch { problem = MacError.unreachable.message }
+    }
+    private func addPhotos() async {
+        describing = false
+        await run("photos") { photos += try await store.mac.choosePhotos(limit: max(1, 12 - photos.count)) }
+    }
+    private func make() async {
+        guard described else { askedForDescription = true; describing = true; return }
+        describing = false
+        await run("make") {
+            let object = try await store.mac.create(instructions)
+            try await store.mac.sendChosen(object.id, photos)
+            started(try await store.mac.start(object.id))
+        }
+    }
+    private func scan() async {
+        await run("scan") {
+            let object = try await store.mac.create(instructions)
+            if let run = try await store.mac.scan(object.id) { started(try await store.mac.sendScan(object.id, run: run)) }
+            else { started(object) }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+struct ChosenThumbnail: View {
+    let photo: ChosenPhoto
+    let remove: () -> Void
+    var body: some View {
+        ZStack(alignment: .topTrailing) {
+            Group {
+                if let image = UIImage(contentsOfFile: photo.url.path) { Image(uiImage: image).resizable().scaledToFill() }
+                else { Color.secondary.opacity(0.2) }
+            }
+            .frame(width: 84, height: 84)
+            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous))
+            Button(role: .destructive, action: remove) {
+                Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.caption.weight(.bold)).padding(6)
+            }
+            .buttonStyle(.glass)
+            .buttonBorderShape(.circle)
+            .accessibilityLabel("Remove this photo")
+            .offset(x: 6, y: -6)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// MARK: - The object page
+
+struct ObjectDetailView: View {
+    let store: ObjectsStore
+    let id: String
+    let close: () -> Void
+    @State private var note = ""
+    @State private var working = false
+    @State private var problem: String?
+    @State private var confirmDelete = false
+    @State private var plateImage: UIImage?
+    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase
+    private var object: StmObject? { store.objects.first { $0.id == id } }
+
+    var body: some View {
+        Group {
+            if let object { page(object) } else { ProgressView() }
+        }
+        .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
+        .task(id: id) { await reload() }
+        .task(id: (object.map { ObjectWords($0).busy } ?? false) && scenePhase == .active) {
+            guard let object, ObjectWords(object).busy, scenePhase == .active else { return }
+            while !Task.isCancelled {
+                try? await Task.sleep(for: .seconds(3))
+                await reload()
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private func reload() async {
+        do { store.replace(try await store.mac.get(id)) }
+        catch let failure as MacError { problem = failure.message }
+        catch { problem = MacError.unreachable.message }
+    }
+
+    private func act(_ body: @escaping () async throws -> StmObject?) {
+        guard !working else { return }
+        working = true; problem = nil
+        Task {
+            defer { working = false }
+            do { if let next = try await body() { store.replace(next) } else { await reload() } }
+            catch let failure as MacError {
+                if failure.code == "cancelled" { return }
+                if failure.needsPairing { store.onPair(); return }
+                problem = failure.message
+            } catch { problem = MacError.unreachable.message }
+        }
+    }
+
+    @ViewBuilder private func page(_ object: StmObject) -> some View {
+        let words = ObjectWords(object)
+        List {
+            if object.current != nil || words.busy {
+                Section {
+                    Renders(store: store, object: object)
+                        .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets())
+                }
+            }
+            Section {
+                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
+                    if let size = words.size {
+                        Text(size).font(.system(size: 34, weight: .bold, design: .rounded)).monospacedDigit()
+                    }
+                    HStack(spacing: 8) {
+                        if words.busy { ProgressView() }
+                        Text(words.status).font(.body.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(words.tone.color)
+                    }
+                    if let summary = object.current?.summary, !words.busy {
+                        Text(summary).font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(.secondary)
+                    }
+                }
+                .padding(.vertical, 4)
+                if object.plate?.photo == true, let plateImage {
+                    Image(uiImage: plateImage).resizable().scaledToFit()
+                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
+                        .accessibilityLabel("The printer camera's view of the plate")
+                }
+                if let problem { Label(problem, systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill").foregroundStyle(.red) }
+            }
+            if !words.actions.filter({ $0 != .correct }).isEmpty {
+                Section { actions(object, words) }
+                    .listRowBackground(Color.clear)
+                    .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets())
+            }
+            if words.actions.contains(.correct) {
+                Section {
+                    TextField("Say what's wrong, like a size or a missing part", text: $note, axis: .vertical).lineLimit(2...6)
+                    Button("Send Changes") {
+                        let text = note.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+                        act { let next = try await store.mac.correct(object.id, text); note = ""; return next }
+                    }
+                    .disabled(working || note.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty)
+                } header: { Text("Anything to Fix?") } footer: { Text("It goes back to your Mac for another touch-up.") }
+            }
+            Section("What You Asked For") {
+                Text((object.instructions ?? "").isEmpty ? "A copy of what you scanned." : object.instructions!)
+                LabeledContent("Sent", value: ObjectWords.sent(object))
+            }
+        }
+        .navigationTitle(words.title)
+        .toolbar {
+            ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
+                Menu {
+                    if object.current != nil {
+                        Button("Turn It Around", systemImage: "rotate.3d") { act { try await store.mac.showModel(object); return nil } }
+                    }
+                    Button("Delete", systemImage: "trash", role: .destructive) { confirmDelete = true }
+                } label: { Label("More", systemImage: "ellipsis") }
+            }
+        }
+        .confirmationDialog("Delete this from your Mac?", isPresented: $confirmDelete, titleVisibility: .visible) {
+            Button("Delete", role: .destructive) {
+                Task {
+                    do { try await store.mac.delete(object.id); store.remove(object.id); close() }
+                    catch let failure as MacError { problem = failure.message }
+                }
+            }
+        } message: { Text("Its model, photos and renders are removed from your Mac.") }
+        .task(id: "\(object.updatedAtMs)#\(object.plate?.photo == true)") {
+            guard object.plate?.photo == true, let url = try? await store.mac.download(object, "plate.jpg"),
+                  let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url) else { plateImage = nil; return }
+            plateImage = UIImage(data: data)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @ViewBuilder private func actions(_ object: StmObject, _ words: ObjectWords) -> some View {
+        VStack(spacing: 10) {
+            ForEach(Array(words.actions.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, action in
+                switch action {
+                case .print:
+                    big("Print", "printer.fill", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.print(object.id, checked: false) }
+                case .printChecked:
+                    big("It Isn't Printing, Print Again", "printer.fill", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.print(object.id, checked: true) }
+                case .plateClear:
+                    big("It's Actually Clear", "checkmark.circle", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.plateClear(object.id) }
+                case .openCamera:
+                    big("Open Camera", "camera", prominent: true) {
+                        let sent = try await store.mac.addCloseups(object.id, camera: true)
+                        guard sent > 0 else { return nil }
+                        return try await store.mac.closeup(object.id, bestGuess: false)
+                    }
+                case .attachPhotos:
+                    big(object.state == .needsCloseup ? "Attach Photos" : "Add Photos", "photo.on.rectangle", prominent: false) {
+                        let sent = try await store.mac.addCloseups(object.id, camera: false)
+                        guard sent > 0 else { return nil }
+                        if object.state == .needsCloseup { return try await store.mac.closeup(object.id, bestGuess: false) }
+                        return try await store.mac.start(object.id)
+                    }
+                case .bestGuess:
+                    big("Make Your Best Guess", "sparkles", prominent: false) { try await store.mac.closeup(object.id, bestGuess: true) }
+                case .retry:
+                    big("Try Again", "arrow.clockwise", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.start(object.id) }
+                case .finishSending:
+                    big("Finish Sending", "arrow.up.circle", prominent: false) {
+                        if let run = await store.mac.finishedScan(object.id) { return try await store.mac.sendScan(object.id, run: run) }
+                        return try await store.mac.start(object.id)
+                    }
+                case .scan:
+                    if store.canScan {
+                        big("Scan It", "viewfinder", prominent: true) {
+                            guard let run = try await store.mac.scan(object.id) else { return nil }
+                            return try await store.mac.sendScan(object.id, run: run)
+                        }
+                    }
+                case .makeIt:
+                    big("Make It From the Description", "wand.and.sparkles", prominent: false) { try await store.mac.start(object.id) }
+                case .correct:
+                    EmptyView()
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        .padding(.vertical, 4)
+    }
+
+    private func big(_ title: String, _ symbol: String, prominent: Bool, _ body: @escaping () async throws -> StmObject?) -> some View {
+        Group {
+            if prominent {
+                Button { act(body) } label: { Label(title, systemImage: symbol).frame(maxWidth: .infinity) }.buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
+            } else {
+                Button { act(body) } label: { Label(title, systemImage: symbol).frame(maxWidth: .infinity) }.buttonStyle(.glass)
+            }
+        }
+        .controlSize(.large)
+        .disabled(working)
+        .accessibilityLabel(title)
+    }
+}
+
+/// The renders, swipeable, on the dark build plate; the clearest one first (the Mac names them so).
+struct Renders: View {
+    let store: ObjectsStore
+    let object: StmObject
+    var body: some View {
+        let renders = object.current?.renders ?? []
+        ZStack {
+            Color(white: 0.17)
+            if renders.isEmpty {
+                VStack(spacing: 10) {
+                    ProgressView().tint(.white)
+                    Text("The model shows here when it's ready.").font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(.white.opacity(0.8))
+                }
+            } else {
+                TabView {
+                    ForEach(renders, id: \.self) { render in
+                        RenderImage(store: store, object: object, render: render)
+                    }
+                }
+                .tabViewStyle(.page(indexDisplayMode: renders.count > 1 ? .always : .never))
+            }
+        }
+        .aspectRatio(1, contentMode: .fit)
+    }
+}

+// MARK: - Hosting inside the React Native shell
+
+/// The React Native view that shows the SwiftUI app. `pairingKey` changes after pairing, so the app reloads;
+/// `onPair` asks the shell to show its pairing screen.
+@objc(STMObjectsAppView)
+public final class STMObjectsAppView: UIView {
+    @objc public var pairingKey: NSNumber = 0 { didSet { render() } }
+    @objc public var onPair: RCTDirectEventBlock? { didSet { store.onPair = { [weak self] in self?.onPair?([:]) } } }
+    private let store = ObjectsStore()
+    private var host: UIHostingController<ObjectsRootView>?
+
+    public override func didMoveToWindow() {
+        super.didMoveToWindow()
+        guard window != nil, host == nil else { return }
+        let host = UIHostingController(rootView: ObjectsRootView(store: store, pairingKey: pairingKey.intValue))
+        // A child of the nearest view controller, so navigation bars, sheets and dialogs work.
+        var responder: UIResponder? = self
+        while let next = responder?.next, !(next is UIViewController) { responder = next }
+        (responder?.next as? UIViewController)?.addChild(host)
+        host.view.frame = bounds
+        host.view.autoresizingMask = [.flexibleWidth, .flexibleHeight]
+        host.view.backgroundColor = .clear
+        addSubview(host.view)
+        host.didMove(toParent: host.parent)
+        self.host = host
+    }
+    private func render() { host?.rootView = ObjectsRootView(store: store, pairingKey: pairingKey.intValue) }
+    public override func layoutSubviews() { super.layoutSubviews(); host?.view.frame = bounds }
+}

+import Foundation
+
+/// One thing scanned or asked for, as the Mac keeps it (`/v1/objects`). Unknown fields are ignored and an
+/// unknown state from a newer Mac still decodes, so an update on one side never breaks the other.
+struct StmObject: Codable, Identifiable, Equatable, Sendable {
+    enum State: String, Codable, Sendable {
+        case new, touchingUp = "touching-up", needsCloseup = "needs-closeup", ready, printing, printed, failed, unknown
+        init(from decoder: Decoder) throws {
+            self = State(rawValue: try decoder.singleValueContainer().decode(String.self)) ?? .unknown
+        }
+    }
+    struct Pass: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { var startedAtMs: Double; var endedAtMs: Double?; var note: String?; var status: String?; var summary: String? }
+    struct Current: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { var model: String; var renders: [String]; var sizeMm: [Double]?; var summary: String? }
+    struct Question: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { var closeup: String? }
+    struct Plate: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { var item: String?; var noCamera: Bool?; var photo: Bool? }
+    struct Print: Codable, Equatable, Sendable { var bundleId: String; var uncertain: Bool? }
+
+    var id: String
+    var createdAtMs: Double
+    var updatedAtMs: Double
+    var instructions: String?
+    var state: State
+    var scan: String?
+    var photos: [String]
+    var passes: [Pass]
+    var current: Current?
+    var question: Question?
+    var plate: Plate?
+    var failure: String?
+    var print: Print?
+}
+
+/// What the object page and the list can offer for an object right now.
+enum ObjectAction: Equatable, Sendable {
+    case finishSending, scan, attachPhotos, makeIt, print, printChecked, correct, openCamera, bestGuess, plateClear, retry
+}
+
+/// An object in plain words. The copy matches the Mac app's `ObjectWords` and the spec's voice.
+struct ObjectWords: Equatable, Sendable {
+    enum Tone: Sendable { case pending, pass, fail, accent, neutral }
+    let title: String
+    let status: String
+    /// Touching up or printing: the page keeps itself up to date.
+    let busy: Bool
+    let tone: Tone
+    /// "W × D × H mm", whole millimetres (LiDAR isn't sub-millimetre).
+    let size: String?
+    let actions: [ObjectAction]
+
+    static let wait = "This can take a few minutes. We'll let you know when it's ready."
+
+    static func title(_ object: StmObject) -> String {
+        let text = (object.instructions ?? "").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+        guard !text.isEmpty else { return object.scan == nil ? "Something new" : "Copy of your scan" }
+        // The first sentence, without its full stop, cut at a word if it is long.
+        var first = text.components(separatedBy: "\n")[0]
+        if let end = first.range(of: #"[.!?](\s|$)"#, options: .regularExpression) { first = String(first[..<end.lowerBound]) }
+        guard first.count > 44 else { return first }
+        let cut = String(first.prefix(43))
+        let word = cut.range(of: " ", options: .backwards).map { String(cut[..<$0.lowerBound]) } ?? cut
+        return word.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) + "…"
+    }
+
+    static func formatSize(_ size: [Double]) -> String? {
+        guard size.count == 3 else { return nil }
+        return size.map { String(Int($0.rounded())) }.joined(separator: " × ") + " mm"
+    }
+
+    /// What reached the Mac, in a few words.
+    static func sent(_ object: StmObject) -> String {
+        let photos = object.photos.count
+        let photoWords = photos == 1 ? "1 photo" : "\(photos) photos"
+        switch (object.scan != nil, photos) {
+        case (true, 0): return "A scan"
+        case (true, _): return "A scan and \(photoWords)"
+        case (false, 0): return "Just the description"
+        case (false, _): return photoWords.prefix(1).uppercased() + photoWords.dropFirst()
+        }
+    }
+
+    init(_ object: StmObject) {
+        title = Self.title(object)
+        size = object.current?.sizeMm.flatMap(Self.formatSize)
+        let made: [ObjectAction] = object.current == nil ? [] : [object.print?.uncertain == true ? .printChecked : .print, .correct]
+        switch object.state {
+        case .new:
+            if object.scan != nil || !object.photos.isEmpty {
+                (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Sending to your Mac…", .pending, true, [.finishSending])
+            } else {
+                let described = !(object.instructions ?? "").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty
+                (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Waiting for a scan or photos.", .neutral, false,
+                                                 described ? [.scan, .attachPhotos, .makeIt] : [.scan, .attachPhotos])
+            }
+        case .touchingUp:
+            let what = object.passes.count > 1 && object.current != nil ? "Making your changes."
+                : object.scan != nil ? "Touching up your scan." : "Making it."
+            (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("\(what) \(Self.wait)", .pending, true, [])
+        case .needsCloseup:
+            let part = (object.question?.closeup ?? "").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+            (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Can you send me a close-up of \(part.isEmpty ? "the part I missed" : part)? I can't see what's happening there.",
+                                             .accent, false, [.openCamera, .attachPhotos, .bestGuess])
+        case .ready:
+            (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Ready to print", .pass, false, made)
+        case .printing:
+            if object.plate?.noCamera == true {
+                (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Check the plate is clear.", .accent, true, [.plateClear])
+            } else if let plate = object.plate {
+                let item = (plate.item ?? "").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+                (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Looks like there's \(item.isEmpty ? "something" : item) on your plate. Can you take it off first?",
+                                                 .accent, true, [.plateClear])
+            } else {
+                (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Printing", .pending, true, [])
+            }
+        case .printed:
+            (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Printed", .pass, false, made)
+        case .failed:
+            let failure = (object.failure ?? "").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
+            (status, tone, busy, actions) = (failure.isEmpty ? "Something went wrong on your Mac." : failure, .fail, false,
+                                             made.isEmpty ? [.retry] : made)
+        case .unknown:
+            (status, tone, busy, actions) = ("Update Scan to Make to see this one.", .neutral, false, [])
+        }
+    }
+}

+#pragma mark Photos chosen on the start screen
+
+// Photos picked before anything is sent: kept on this phone until Make it, shown from their cache
+// file, then sent to the new object. Their ids are `<uuid>.<ext>` in Caches/ObjectsChosen.
+static NSURL *STMObjectsChosenRoot(void) { return STMObjectsRoot(NSCachesDirectory, @"ObjectsChosen"); }
+
+RCT_REMAP_METHOD(choosePhotos, choosePhotosOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
+  NSInteger limit = [o[@"limit"] integerValue];
+  UIViewController *presenter = RCTPresentedViewController();
+  if (limit < 1 || limit > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
+  if ([self sheetBusy:presenter]) { reject(@"busy", @"Close the current sheet first.", nil); return; }
+  PHPickerConfiguration *configuration = [[PHPickerConfiguration alloc] init];
+  configuration.filter = [PHPickerFilter imagesFilter]; configuration.selectionLimit = limit;
+  configuration.preferredAssetRepresentationMode = PHPickerConfigurationAssetRepresentationModeCurrent;
+  PHPickerViewController *picker = [[PHPickerViewController alloc] initWithConfiguration:configuration];
+  picker.delegate = self; picker.presentationController.delegate = self;
+  self.pickObject = nil; self.pickResolve = resolve; self.pickReject = reject;
+  [presenter presentViewController:picker animated:YES completion:nil];
+}
+
+/** Keeps chosen photos in the cache and resolves their ids and display URIs. */
+- (void)finishChosen:(NSArray<NSDictionary *> *)photos {
+  RCTPromiseResolveBlock resolve = self.pickResolve; self.pickResolve = nil; self.pickReject = nil;
+  if (!resolve) return;
+  NSURL *root = STMObjectsChosenRoot();
+  [NSFileManager.defaultManager createDirectoryAtURL:root withIntermediateDirectories:YES attributes:nil error:nil];
+  NSMutableArray *chosen = [NSMutableArray array];
+  for (NSDictionary *photo in photos) {
+    NSString *name = [NSString stringWithFormat:@"%@.%@", NSUUID.UUID.UUIDString.lowercaseString, [photo[@"name"] pathExtension]];
+    NSURL *url = [root URLByAppendingPathComponent:name];
+    if ([photo[@"bytes"] writeToURL:url options:NSDataWritingAtomic error:nil]) [chosen addObject:@{@"id": name, @"uri": url.absoluteString}];
+  }
+  resolve(chosen);
+}
+
+RCT_REMAP_METHOD(sendChosen, sendChosenOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
+  NSString *objectId = o[@"objectId"]; NSArray *ids = o[@"ids"];
+  if (!STMObjectsMatches(objectId, STMObjectsUUID) || ![ids isKindOfClass:NSArray.class] || ids.count > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
+  NSMutableArray *files = [NSMutableArray array];
+  for (id chosen in ids) {
+    if (!STMObjectsMatches(chosen, @"[a-f0-9-]{36}\\.(jpg|png|heic)")) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
+    NSString *ext = [chosen pathExtension];
+    NSString *type = [ext isEqual:@"png"] ? @"image/png" : [ext isEqual:@"heic"] ? @"image/heic" : @"image/jpeg";
+    [files addObject:@{@"name": [@"photo-" stringByAppendingString:chosen], @"url": [STMObjectsChosenRoot() URLByAppendingPathComponent:chosen], @"type": type}];
+  }
+  if (!files.count) { resolve(@{@"photosSent": @0}); return; }
+  [self sendFiles:files objectId:objectId resolve:^{
+    for (NSDictionary *file in files) [NSFileManager.defaultManager removeItemAtURL:file[@"url"] error:nil];
+    resolve(@{@"photosSent": @(files.count)});
+  } reject:reject];
+}
+

   480	    private func run(_ kind: String, _ body: () async throws -> Void) async {
   481	        guard working == nil else { return }
   482	        working = kind; problem = nil
   483	        defer { working = nil }
   484	        do { try await body() }
   485	        catch let failure as MacError {
   486	            if failure.code == "cancelled" { return }
   487	            if failure.needsPairing { dismiss(); store.onPair(); return }
   488	            problem = failure.message
   489	        } catch { problem = MacError.unreachable.message }
   490	    }
   491	    private func addPhotos() async {
   492	        describing = false
   493	        await run("photos") { photos += try await store.mac.choosePhotos(limit: max(1, 12 - photos.count)) }
   494	    }
   495	    private func make() async {
   496	        guard described else { askedForDescription = true; describing = true; return }
   497	        describing = false
   498	        await run("make") {
   499	            let object = try await store.mac.create(instructions)
   500	            try await store.mac.sendChosen(object.id, photos)

# Scan to Make: rebuild the app around scan → touch-up → print

Status: draft for approval. Supersedes the user-facing parts of `docs/design/journey.md` where they conflict.

## 1. Intent

Scan to Make turns a LiDAR scan of a real object into a model that matches the object, then prints it. Printing is already solved by bambu-printer-mcp and mcp-3D-printer-server. The app's own job is everything around that:
- capturing the object;
- touching up the scan from its photos;
- getting the result to the printer without the user hunting for anything.

Every screen the user sees is rebuilt from first principles. Two parts work well and stay: the QR pairing between phone and Mac, and the LiDAR capture.

## 2. What it is

Two jobs, one flow:

- **Make something that fits or attaches.** This is the main use: a hook for a handle, a holder for a cup, a mount that joins a product to an arm or stand. You give it a scan of the thing you have, or just a photo or two, plus exact instructions. When a product's dimensions are published, Opus looks them up instead of needing a scan.
- **Copy an object.** You scan a thing, and Opus fixes what the scanner got wrong, using the photos from that scan.

Either way, your Mac's Opus does the work in Blender through your printer MCP. Your phone tells you when it's ready and shows you exactly what will print and its size, and you tap Print. If something is wrong, you say so in a sentence and it goes again.

## 3. The rule for code

This app contains only what the MCPs can't provide:
- capture;
- pairing;
- the screens;
- orchestration: running the user's Claude with their MCP, and moving files and state between phone and Mac.

Every mesh, Blender, slicing and printer operation goes through bambu-printer-mcp or mcp-3D-printer-server.

**Attach to the user's installed MCP. Never ship a copy.** The printer MCPs update several times a week to keep up with new printers, firmware and slicers. An app release, and App Store review in particular, can't keep pace. So the app never bundles, pins or installs its own copy of a printer MCP. It launches the one installed on the user's computer, found from the user's own agent configuration, so every MCP update reaches the app the moment the user has it. That includes the app's ability to touch up and print. All printer logic lives on the Mac side, behind the MCP. The iPhone app contains none. If a needed capability is missing, it is added to the MCP under that repo's own rules. It is never written a second time here.

## 4. Keep, rebuild, delete

**Keep:**
- **Pairing:** the QR flow end to end. That's the Mac's QR window, the phone's viewfinder and pairing, auth v2, pairing payload v2, and the pinned-certificate LAN transport.
- **LiDAR capture:** the native Object Capture module (`apps/mobile/modules/object-capture`) and LiDAR gating.

**Rebuild from scratch:** every screen and user-visible string on the iPhone (`apps/mobile/src/screens/**`, `App.tsx` navigation) and on the Mac (`apps/mac/App/**`, except the QR window).

**Delete once replaced:**
- the current phone screens and the open PRs that extend them;
- the Mac first-run, settings, model sign-in and nearby-pairing UI;
- `tools/scan-correct`, `tools/ai-tweak-judge`, `tools/scan-repair`, `tools/capture-mesh-convert`. Their jobs move to Opus working through the printer MCP's Blender bridge. Anything deterministic that's still needed moves into the printer MCPs;
- the maker's-size lookup and the product presets;
- the app's bundled, pinned copy of bambu-printer-mcp, and any plan to install pinned MCP versions.

Each PR lists what it deletes.

## 5. The experience

### 5.1 Objects, on both apps

Both apps show the same list: one card per scanned object, showing its current model and its state in plain words. Tapping a card opens the **object page**, which is the same on iPhone and Mac.

States are in plain words. The builder writes the copy, in this voice:
- "Sending to your Mac…"
- "Touching up your scan. This can take a few minutes. We'll let you know when it's ready."
- "Need a close-up of ___" (5.4)
- "Ready to print"
- "Printing, 40%"
- "Printed"
- Failures, each in one sentence with the one action that fixes it.

### 5.2 Starting something (iPhone)

The list has one obvious way to start. It opens on a box for instructions ("what do you want to make?", which can be left empty for a plain copy), and the user then adds either:
- a **scan**: the LiDAR capture;
- **photos**: a photo or two, or none when the instructions name a product with published dimensions.

The instructions are used exactly as written. They can include product names, model numbers and pasted links ("a mount for this mic on this arm: <link> <link>"), which is how exact products get in when a shop blocks lookups.

**Scan** opens the LiDAR capture. When the scan ends, the phone sends the model **and the photos Object Capture took** to the Mac by itself. There is no send button, photo step or size step. If sending can't happen, the phone says why and what to do.

Devices without LiDAR run the app fully but can't scan. They say scanning needs a LiDAR device. There is no photo-only substitute. iPad and the foldable iPhone get real layouts: folded is the iPhone layout, open is two panes, and iPad is sidebar plus detail.

### 5.3 Ready

When the touch-up finishes, the phone gets a notification naming the object. Tapping it opens the object page:
- the touched-up model, turnable in 3D: exactly what will print;
- its size, from LiDAR ("W × D × H mm, ready to print"). The size is never asked for;
- **Print**;
- a corrections box. A note such as "you missed the clip on the side" or "it's 70 mm long" goes back to the touch-up, the state returns to touching up, and the user is notified again. Needing more than one round is a touch-up quality problem, not something the UI should absorb.

### 5.4 When the touch-up needs more

The touch-up works from the scan's own photos. Only if it can't see something does the object ask, in one sentence: "Can you send me a close-up of ___? I can't see what's happening there." It offers three buttons:
- **Open camera**
- **Attach photos**
- **Make your best guess**: the touch-up fills in the gap and carries on.

### 5.5 Print

**Print** prints, through bambu-printer-mcp or mcp-3D-printer-server via the existing `services/printer-handoff` contract.

- **Bed check:** it runs in the background through the printer's camera. The user never sees a photo of an empty plate.
- **Only when something is really on the plate** does the phone show the photo: "Looks like there's a ___ on your plate. Can you take it off first?" It also offers an override, **"It's actually clear"**, for printer parts, reflections and odd light.
- **Confirmation:** the Print tap plus a passed or overridden check answers the MCP's own confirmation.
- **Printers with no camera** show one line before printing: "Check the plate is clear."
- **Afterwards:** progress, then a "done" notification.

### 5.6 The Mac app

A menu-bar app whose window shows the same object list and object pages, plus the QR window for pairing. v1 has no setup wizard, settings maze or model sign-in sheet. It uses what the user already has (§7).

## 6. The engine on the Mac

1. **Receive** the scan's model and photos into a fresh folder for that object.
2. **Touch up** by running the user's own Claude Code login (`claude -p`, Opus 5.5, effort `high`) with one MCP server: **the user's printer MCP**, whichever they use (bambu-printer-mcp or mcp-3D-printer-server). Both have a built-in Blender MCP bridge (discover, forward calls such as `execute_blender_code`, verified STL edits and export), enabled in that MCP's own config. Blender is reached only through that bridge, and this app never wires a second Blender path. The worker uses whatever tool names that MCP exposes, and must work with either.
   - **Input:** whatever the user gave, as is:
     - the scan as captured, with its colour texture, if there is one;
     - its photos or the photos the user added;
     - the user's instructions.

     With no instructions, the instruction is "Look at the pictures of this, and then tweak the scan based on that." Making something new from instructions is the same pass, with the same tools and guard rails. The app adds no design code; Opus designs in Blender through the printer MCP.
   - **Web lookup:** the worker may look up published dimensions of named products on the web. That's the only tool it gets besides Read and the printer MCP.
   - **Output:** an STL plus renders.
   - **Behaviour:**
     - **Targeted edits only**, like a brush: restore surface the scanner smoothed over, add a small feature it missed, flatten a surface that light or movement left noisy. Everything the scanner captured well stays as it is.
     - Use Blender's own tools rather than writing new geometry code.
     - **One pass, then stop:** make the fixes the photos show, save, and stop. No polishing passes.
     - **No print prep:** bases, orientation and supports belong to the MCP at print time.
     - **Never smooth** surface the scanner really captured.
   - **Guard rails (enforced by the app):**
     - a checkpoint after each fix, so a later bad change can't lose a good result;
     - a hard cap on turns and time;
     - a fresh, empty Blender scene per touch-up;
     - a working folder holding only that object's files, because Blender's Python can read the whole disk.
   - **Isolation:** the worker runs isolated from the user's personal Claude setup:
     - only Read and web lookup among the built-in tools;
     - no skills or slash commands;
     - no personal instructions, memory or hooks;
     - only the user's printer MCP.

     The builder finds the cleanest way to do that while keeping the user's own login.
   - **Questions:** if the touch-up needs a close-up, it asks as a structured question the app can show (5.4).
   - **Corrections:** each note starts a new pass from the current model with a short summary, not a replay of the whole previous session.
3. **Print** by calling the user's printer MCP unchanged with its existing config, using that MCP's own slice and print tools and its camera tool for the bed check where the printer has one.

**The app inherits the MCP's config** and never keeps its own copy of printer settings. Where the app trips on the MCP's normal behaviour, the fix goes into the MCP when it benefits every user (for example, always returning absolute output paths). Otherwise the app adapts. The user's config is never overridden.

**The app keeps Blender ready.** The bridge needs Blender running with the Blender MCP addon's server started. The Mac app launches Blender (hidden where possible) with a startup script that enables the addon and starts its server.

## 7. Users who don't have the tools yet

v1 targets people who already have a printer MCP (bambu-printer-mcp or mcp-3D-printer-server), Claude, and Blender with Blender MCP. If something is missing or a user wants to change it, the Mac app sets it up itself: install, connect, check, with plain progress. That follows once the core loop works.

## 8. Constraints

- iOS 27 baseline. LiDAR is required to scan.
- Build, install and drive the iPhone app with T3 Code's device tools (`agent-device`, the T3 device hub), not Xcode workflows.
- The phone stays a React Native shell with native modules (pairing and capture live there). The Mac app stays SwiftUI.
- AGENTS.md rules hold: read the MCP repos before touching printing, add no invented restrictions, keep credentials and agent attribution out of Git, run local CI before pushing.
- Judge every touch-up by its renders against its photos, never by metrics.
- Notifications go through Apple's push service, sent directly from the Mac with a push key. No hosted server.
- v1 works on the home network, or over the user's own Tailscale. No hosted relay.

## 9. Success

Someone scans an object and puts the phone down. They get "ready to print", see a model that matches the object at its real size, tap Print, and it prints, with no confusing screen along the way.

- The first touch-up fixes what the photos make obvious.
- Something made to fit or attach fits the real thing on the first print, from a scan, from a photo or two, or from published dimensions alone.
- At most one correction round for typical objects.
- A touch-up pass takes a few minutes. Time and usage per object are measured and reported in each PR that changes the touch-up.

## 10. Non-goals and anti-goals

**Non-goals:**
- our own slicer, printability gate, print client, or design/template engine;
- printer setup UI for users who already have the MCPs;
- a photo-only scan for non-LiDAR devices.

**Anti-goals:**
- any screen where the user hunts for Print;
- a separate photo or size step;
- a photo of an empty plate;
- flagging printer parts as obstructions;
- asking for anything the app or the MCP config already knows;
- a touch-up that returns the scan unchanged and calls it done;
- a touch-up that keeps going after it's done and makes things worse;
- process-heavy status text.

## 11. Milestones and the builder's calls

1. **Prove the touch-up first, before any UI.** Run it end to end through a printer MCP's Blender bridge only (both MCPs), under the guard rails in §6, using only the photos Object Capture took during the scan. Those photos currently stay on the phone in the capture's `Images/` folder; only the model is sent. If they aren't enough for a good result, change what is captured or sent before building screens. "Need a close-up" must stay the exception.
   Also prove the main job: make an attachment for a real product from a photo or two plus instructions, using its published dimensions, with no scan.
2. Phone and Mac object list and object page, scan → send → touch-up → ready → print, with notifications.
3. Setup for users missing a tool (§7).

Left to the builder:
- visual design, layout and copy in the voice above, by the qualified UI author;
- how object state syncs between Mac and phone (the existing authenticated LAN transport is available);
- which capture photos are sent and at what size;
- 3D viewing on both apps;
- the order of deletions, so the app always has a working path.

+#pragma mark Photos chosen on the start screen
+
+// Photos picked before anything is sent: kept on this phone until Make it, shown from their cache
+// file, then sent to the new object. Their ids are `<uuid>.<ext>` in Caches/ObjectsChosen.
+static NSURL *STMObjectsChosenRoot(void) { return STMObjectsRoot(NSCachesDirectory, @"ObjectsChosen"); }
+
+RCT_REMAP_METHOD(choosePhotos, choosePhotosOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
+  NSInteger limit = [o[@"limit"] integerValue];
+  UIViewController *presenter = RCTPresentedViewController();
+  if (limit < 1 || limit > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
+  if ([self sheetBusy:presenter]) { reject(@"busy", @"Close the current sheet first.", nil); return; }
+  PHPickerConfiguration *configuration = [[PHPickerConfiguration alloc] init];
+  configuration.filter = [PHPickerFilter imagesFilter]; configuration.selectionLimit = limit;
+  configuration.preferredAssetRepresentationMode = PHPickerConfigurationAssetRepresentationModeCurrent;
+  PHPickerViewController *picker = [[PHPickerViewController alloc] initWithConfiguration:configuration];
+  picker.delegate = self; picker.presentationController.delegate = self;
+  self.pickObject = nil; self.pickResolve = resolve; self.pickReject = reject;
+  [presenter presentViewController:picker animated:YES completion:nil];
+}
+
+/** Keeps chosen photos in the cache and resolves their ids and display URIs. */
+- (void)finishChosen:(NSArray<NSDictionary *> *)photos {
+  RCTPromiseResolveBlock resolve = self.pickResolve; self.pickResolve = nil; self.pickReject = nil;
+  if (!resolve) return;
+  NSURL *root = STMObjectsChosenRoot();
+  [NSFileManager.defaultManager createDirectoryAtURL:root withIntermediateDirectories:YES attributes:nil error:nil];
+  NSMutableArray *chosen = [NSMutableArray array];
+  for (NSDictionary *photo in photos) {
+    NSString *name = [NSString stringWithFormat:@"%@.%@", NSUUID.UUID.UUIDString.lowercaseString, [photo[@"name"] pathExtension]];
+    NSURL *url = [root URLByAppendingPathComponent:name];
+    if ([photo[@"bytes"] writeToURL:url options:NSDataWritingAtomic error:nil]) [chosen addObject:@{@"id": name, @"uri": url.absoluteString}];
+  }
+  resolve(chosen);
+}
+
+RCT_REMAP_METHOD(sendChosen, sendChosenOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
+  NSString *objectId = o[@"objectId"]; NSArray *ids = o[@"ids"];
+  if (!STMObjectsMatches(objectId, STMObjectsUUID) || ![ids isKindOfClass:NSArray.class] || ids.count > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
+  NSMutableArray *files = [NSMutableArray array];
+  for (id chosen in ids) {
+    if (!STMObjectsMatches(chosen, @"[a-f0-9-]{36}\\.(jpg|png|heic)")) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
+    NSString *ext = [chosen pathExtension];
+    NSString *type = [ext isEqual:@"png"] ? @"image/png" : [ext isEqual:@"heic"] ? @"image/heic" : @"image/jpeg";
+    [files addObject:@{@"name": [@"photo-" stringByAppendingString:chosen], @"url": [STMObjectsChosenRoot() URLByAppendingPathComponent:chosen], @"type": type}];
+  }
+  if (!files.count) { resolve(@{@"photosSent": @0}); return; }
+  [self sendFiles:files objectId:objectId resolve:^{
+    for (NSDictionary *file in files) [NSFileManager.defaultManager removeItemAtURL:file[@"url"] error:nil];
+    resolve(@{@"photosSent": @(files.count)});
+  } reject:reject];
+}

   480	    private func run(_ kind: String, _ body: () async throws -> Void) async {
   481	        guard working == nil else { return }
   482	        working = kind; problem = nil
   483	        defer { working = nil }
   484	        do { try await body() }
   485	        catch let failure as MacError {
   486	            if failure.code == "cancelled" { return }
   487	            if failure.needsPairing { dismiss(); store.onPair(); return }
   488	            problem = failure.message
   489	        } catch { problem = MacError.unreachable.message }
   490	    }
