    11	// Objects on the paired Mac (/v1/objects). Pairing credentials stay native and
    12	// every request is pinned like the other bridges. Files go straight from the
    13	// capture folder, the photo picker or the camera to the Mac, so JavaScript
    14	// never handles file paths; downloads come back as cached file URIs.
    15	
    16	static NSString *const STMObjectsUUID = @"[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}";
    17	static NSUInteger const STMObjectsScanLimit = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
    18	static NSUInteger const STMObjectsPhotoLimit = 25 * 1024 * 1024;
    19	static NSUInteger const STMObjectsJSONLimit = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
    20	
    21	static BOOL STMObjectsMatches(id value, NSString *pattern) {
    22	  return [value isKindOfClass:NSString.class] && [[NSPredicate predicateWithFormat:@"SELF MATCHES %@", pattern] evaluateWithObject:value];
    23	}
    24	static NSString *STMObjectsPath(NSString *path, NSString *method) {
    25	  // Only the objects routes, each with its own method.
    26	  NSString *one = [NSString stringWithFormat:@"/v1/objects/%@", STMObjectsUUID];
    27	  if ([method isEqual:@"GET"] && (STMObjectsMatches(path, @"/v1/objects") || STMObjectsMatches(path, one))) return @"projects:read";
    28	  if ([method isEqual:@"DELETE"] && STMObjectsMatches(path, one)) return @"captures:import";
    29	  if ([method isEqual:@"POST"] && STMObjectsMatches(path, @"/v1/objects")) return @"captures:import";
    30	  if ([method isEqual:@"POST"] && STMObjectsMatches(path, [one stringByAppendingString:@"/(start|corrections|closeup)"])) return @"captures:import";
    31	  if ([method isEqual:@"POST"] && STMObjectsMatches(path, [one stringByAppendingString:@"/(print|plate)"])) return @"printer-handoff:use";
    32	  return nil;
    33	}
    34	/** The record, if it is current: unexpired and holding the scope. */
    35	static NSDictionary *STMObjectsPairing(NSString *scope, NSString **code) {
    36	  NSError *error = nil;
    37	  NSDictionary *pairing = STMReadPairing(&error);
    38	  if (error || !pairing || !STMPairingBaseURL(pairing) || [pairing[@"expiresAt"] doubleValue] <= NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970 * 1000) { *code = @"pairing-required"; return nil; }
    39	  if (scope && ![pairing[@"scopes"] containsObject:scope]) { *code = @"scope-denied"; return nil; }
    40	  return pairing;
    41	}
    42	static NSURL *STMObjectsRoot(NSSearchPathDirectory directory, NSString *name) {
    43	  return [[[NSFileManager.defaultManager URLsForDirectory:directory inDomains:NSUserDomainMask] firstObject] URLByAppendingPathComponent:name isDirectory:YES];
    44	}
    45	/** Evenly spread picks from first to last, so every side of the object stays represented. */
    46	static NSArray *STMObjectsSpread(NSArray *items, NSUInteger count) {
    47	  if (items.count <= count) return items;
    48	  NSMutableArray *picked = [NSMutableArray arrayWithCapacity:count];
    49	  for (NSUInteger i = 0; i < count; i++) [picked addObject:items[(NSUInteger)llround((double)i * (items.count - 1) / (count - 1))]];
    50	  return picked;
    51	}
    52	
    53	/** One pinned request with its own session. */
    54	@interface STMObjectsRequest : NSObject <NSURLSessionDataDelegate>
    55	@property NSDictionary *pairing;
    56	@property NSUInteger limit;
    57	@property NSMutableData *received;
    58	@property NSHTTPURLResponse *response;
    59	@property BOOL pinRejected;
    60	@property BOOL tooLarge;
    61	@property(copy) void (^done)(NSInteger status, NSData *body, NSString *code);
    62	@end
    63	@implementation STMObjectsRequest
    64	+ (void)send:(NSString *)method path:(NSString *)path body:(NSData *)body type:(NSString *)type pairing:(NSDictionary *)pairing limit:(NSUInteger)limit
    65	        done:(void (^)(NSInteger status, NSData *body, NSString *code))done {
    66	  STMObjectsRequest *request = [STMObjectsRequest new];
    67	  request.pairing = pairing; request.limit = limit; request.received = [NSMutableData new]; request.done = done;
    68	  NSMutableURLRequest *http = [NSMutableURLRequest requestWithURL:[NSURL URLWithString:path relativeToURL:STMPairingBaseURL(pairing)].absoluteURL];
    69	  http.HTTPMethod = method; http.HTTPBody = body;
    70	  [http setValue:[@"Bearer " stringByAppendingString:pairing[@"token"]] forHTTPHeaderField:@"Authorization"];
    71	  [http setValue:pairing[@"deviceId"] forHTTPHeaderField:@"X-Scan-Device"];
    72	  if (body) [http setValue:type forHTTPHeaderField:@"Content-Type"];
    73	  NSURLSessionConfiguration *config = NSURLSessionConfiguration.ephemeralSessionConfiguration;
    74	  config.URLCache = nil; config.HTTPShouldSetCookies = NO;
    75	  // Scans are tens of megabytes over Wi-Fi.
    76	  config.timeoutIntervalForRequest = 90; config.timeoutIntervalForResource = body.length > STMObjectsPhotoLimit ? 600 : 180;
    77	  NSURLSession *session = [NSURLSession sessionWithConfiguration:config delegate:request delegateQueue:NSOperationQueue.mainQueue];
    78	  [[session dataTaskWithRequest:http] resume];
    79	  [session finishTasksAndInvalidate];
    80	}
    81	- (void)URLSession:(NSURLSession *)s task:(NSURLSessionTask *)t willPerformHTTPRedirection:(NSHTTPURLResponse *)r newRequest:(NSURLRequest *)q completionHandler:(void (^)(NSURLRequest *))done { done(nil); }
    82	- (void)URLSession:(NSURLSession *)s didReceiveChallenge:(NSURLAuthenticationChallenge *)c completionHandler:(void (^)(NSURLSessionAuthChallengeDisposition, NSURLCredential *))done {
    83	  if (STMPinnedTrust(c, self.pairing, done)) self.pinRejected = YES;
    84	}
    85	- (void)URLSession:(NSURLSession *)s dataTask:(NSURLSessionDataTask *)t didReceiveResponse:(NSURLResponse *)r completionHandler:(void (^)(NSURLSessionResponseDisposition))done {
    86	  self.response = (NSHTTPURLResponse *)r;
    87	  if (self.response.expectedContentLength > (long long)self.limit) { self.tooLarge = YES; done(NSURLSessionResponseCancel); return; }
    88	  done(NSURLSessionResponseAllow);
    89	}
    90	- (void)URLSession:(NSURLSession *)s dataTask:(NSURLSessionDataTask *)t didReceiveData:(NSData *)d {
    91	  if (d.length > self.limit - MIN(self.limit, self.received.length)) { self.tooLarge = YES; [t cancel]; return; }
    92	  [self.received appendData:d];
    93	}
    94	- (void)URLSession:(NSURLSession *)s task:(NSURLSessionTask *)t didCompleteWithError:(NSError *)e {
    95	  void (^done)(NSInteger, NSData *, NSString *) = self.done; self.done = nil;
    96	  if (!done) return;
    97	  if (self.tooLarge) { done(0, nil, @"payload-too-large"); return; }
    98	  if (e) { done(0, nil, self.pinRejected ? @"certificate-changed" : @"unreachable"); return; }
    99	  NSError *pe = nil; NSDictionary *now = STMReadPairing(&pe);
   100	  if (pe || !STMSamePairing(now, self.pairing)) { done(0, nil, @"pairing-changed"); return; }
   101	  done(self.response.statusCode, self.received, nil);
   102	}
   103	@end
   104	
   105	@interface ObjectsBridge : NSObject <RCTBridgeModule, PHPickerViewControllerDelegate, UIImagePickerControllerDelegate, UINavigationControllerDelegate, UIAdaptivePresentationControllerDelegate>
   106	@property NSMutableSet<NSString *> *uploading;
   107	@property NSString *pickObject;
   108	@property(copy) RCTPromiseResolveBlock pickResolve;
   109	@property(copy) RCTPromiseRejectBlock pickReject;
   110	@end
   111	
   112	@implementation ObjectsBridge
   113	RCT_EXPORT_MODULE()
   114	+ (BOOL)requiresMainQueueSetup { return NO; }
   115	- (dispatch_queue_t)methodQueue { return dispatch_get_main_queue(); }
   116	- (instancetype)init { if ((self = [super init])) _uploading = [NSMutableSet set]; return self; }
   117	
   118	static NSString *STMObjectsMessage(NSString *code) {
   119	  NSDictionary *messages = @{
   120	    @"pairing-required": @"Connect to your Mac again.", @"scope-denied": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
   121	    @"pairing-changed": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
   122	    @"certificate-changed": @"Your Mac presented a different certificate. Scan a new QR code from it.",
   123	    @"unreachable": @"Your Mac isn't answering. Check it's on and awake, then try again.",
   124	    @"payload-too-large": @"That file is too large to send.",
   125	    @"invalid-request": @"That request isn't allowed.",
   126	  };
   127	  return messages[code] ?: @"Something went wrong talking to your Mac. Try again.";
   128	}
   129	
   130	RCT_REMAP_METHOD(call, callOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
   131	  NSString *method = o[@"method"], *path = o[@"path"], *scope = STMObjectsPath(path, method), *code = nil;
   132	  id json = o[@"json"];
   133	  if (!scope || ([method isEqual:@"GET"] && json)) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
   134	  NSDictionary *pairing = STMObjectsPairing(scope, &code);
   135	  if (!pairing) { reject(code, STMObjectsMessage(code), nil); return; }
   136	  NSData *body = nil;
   137	  if (json) {
   138	    if (![NSJSONSerialization isValidJSONObject:json]) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
   139	    body = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:json options:0 error:nil];
   140	    if (!body || body.length > 16 * 1024) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
   141	  }
   142	  [STMObjectsRequest send:method path:path body:body type:@"application/json" pairing:pairing limit:STMObjectsJSONLimit done:^(NSInteger status, NSData *data, NSString *failure) {
   143	    if (failure) { reject(failure, STMObjectsMessage(failure), nil); return; }
   144	    NSString *text = data.length ? [[NSString alloc] initWithData:data encoding:NSUTF8StringEncoding] : @"";
   145	    if (!text) { reject(@"invalid-response", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-response"), nil); return; }
   146	    resolve(@{@"status": @(status), @"body": text});
   147	  }];
   148	}
   149	
   150	/** Sends files one after another; stops at the first failure. */
   151	- (void)upload:(NSArray<NSDictionary *> *)files objectId:(NSString *)objectId pairing:(NSDictionary *)pairing index:(NSUInteger)index sent:(NSUInteger)sent
   152	          done:(void (^)(NSUInteger sent, NSString *code, NSString *message))done {
   153	  if (index >= files.count) { done(sent, nil, nil); return; }
   154	  NSDictionary *file = files[index];
   155	  NSData *bytes = file[@"bytes"] ?: [NSData dataWithContentsOfURL:file[@"url"] options:NSDataReadingMappedIfSafe error:nil];
   156	  NSUInteger limit = [file[@"scan"] boolValue] ? STMObjectsScanLimit : STMObjectsPhotoLimit;
   157	  if (!bytes.length || bytes.length > limit) { done(sent, @"unreadable-file", @"A file from this scan couldn't be read."); return; }
   158	  NSString *path = [file[@"scan"] boolValue] ? [NSString stringWithFormat:@"/v1/objects/%@/scan", objectId]
   159	    : [NSString stringWithFormat:@"/v1/objects/%@/photos?name=%@", objectId, [file[@"name"] stringByAddingPercentEncodingWithAllowedCharacters:NSCharacterSet.URLQueryAllowedCharacterSet]];
   160	  [STMObjectsRequest send:[file[@"scan"] boolValue] ? @"PUT" : @"POST" path:path body:bytes type:file[@"type"] pairing:pairing limit:STMObjectsJSONLimit
   161	                     done:^(NSInteger status, NSData *data, NSString *failure) {
   162	    if (failure) { done(sent, failure, STMObjectsMessage(failure)); return; }
   163	    if (status == 409) { done(sent, @"conflict", @"This is already being touched up."); return; }
   164	    if (status < 200 || status >= 300) { done(sent, status == 404 ? @"not-found" : @"upload-failed", @"Your Mac didn't accept a file from this scan. Try again."); return; }
   165	    [self upload:files objectId:objectId pairing:pairing index:index + 1 sent:sent + ([file[@"scan"] boolValue] ? 0 : 1) done:done];
   166	  }];
   167	}
   168	
   169	/** What the Mac already has, so a resumed send skips it. */
   170	- (void)existing:(NSString *)objectId pairing:(NSDictionary *)pairing done:(void (^)(NSDictionary *object, NSString *code))done {
   171	  [STMObjectsRequest send:@"GET" path:[@"/v1/objects/" stringByAppendingString:objectId] body:nil type:nil pairing:pairing limit:STMObjectsJSONLimit done:^(NSInteger status, NSData *data, NSString *failure) {
   172	    if (failure) { done(nil, failure); return; }
   173	    NSDictionary *body = status == 200 ? [NSJSONSerialization JSONObjectWithData:data options:0 error:nil] : nil;
   174	    NSDictionary *object = [body isKindOfClass:NSDictionary.class] ? body[@"object"] : nil;
   175	    done([object isKindOfClass:NSDictionary.class] ? object : nil, object ? nil : (status == 404 ? @"not-found" : @"upload-failed"));
   176	  }];
   177	}
   178	
   179	/** Sends whatever of these files the Mac doesn't have yet, so a send cut short can simply run again. */
   180	- (void)sendFiles:(NSArray<NSDictionary *> *)files objectId:(NSString *)objectId resolve:(void (^)(void))resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject {
   181	  NSString *code = nil;
   182	  NSDictionary *pairing = STMObjectsPairing(@"captures:import", &code);
   183	  if (!pairing) { reject(code, STMObjectsMessage(code), nil); return; }
   184	  if ([self.uploading containsObject:objectId]) { reject(@"busy", @"This is already being sent.", nil); return; }
   185	  [self.uploading addObject:objectId];
   186	  [self existing:objectId pairing:pairing done:^(NSDictionary *object, NSString *failure) {
   187	    if (!object) { [self.uploading removeObject:objectId]; reject(failure, STMObjectsMessage(failure), nil); return; }
   188	    NSArray *have = [object[@"photos"] isKindOfClass:NSArray.class] ? object[@"photos"] : @[];
   189	    BOOL hasScan = [object[@"scan"] isKindOfClass:NSString.class];
   190	    NSMutableArray *missing = [NSMutableArray array];
   191	    for (NSDictionary *file in files) {
   192	      if ([file[@"scan"] boolValue] ? !hasScan : ![have containsObject:file[@"name"]]) [missing addObject:file];
   193	    }
   194	    [self upload:missing objectId:objectId pairing:pairing index:0 sent:0 done:^(NSUInteger sent, NSString *uploadFailure, NSString *message) {
   195	      [self.uploading removeObject:objectId];
   196	      if (uploadFailure) reject(uploadFailure, message, nil);
   197	      else resolve();
   198	    }];
   199	  }];
   200	}
   201	
   202	RCT_REMAP_METHOD(sendCapture, sendCaptureOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
   203	  NSString *objectId = o[@"objectId"], *runID = o[@"runID"];
   204	  NSInteger maxPhotos = [o[@"maxPhotos"] integerValue];
   205	  if (!STMObjectsMatches(objectId, STMObjectsUUID) || !STMObjectsMatches(runID, STMObjectsUUID) || maxPhotos < 0 || maxPhotos > 48) {
   206	    reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return;
   207	  }
   208	  NSURL *run = [STMObjectsRoot(NSDocumentDirectory, @"NativeCaptures") URLByAppendingPathComponent:runID isDirectory:YES];
   209	  NSURL *model = [run URLByAppendingPathComponent:@"Models/model-mobile.usdz"];
   210	  NSNumber *regular = nil;
   211	  if (![model getResourceValue:&regular forKey:NSURLIsRegularFileKey error:nil] || !regular.boolValue) {
   212	    reject(@"no-model", @"The scan didn't finish making a model. Scan it again.", nil); return;
   213	  }
   214	  NSArray<NSString *> *names = [[NSFileManager.defaultManager contentsOfDirectoryAtPath:[run URLByAppendingPathComponent:@"Images"].path error:nil]
   215	    filteredArrayUsingPredicate:[NSPredicate predicateWithFormat:@"SELF MATCHES %@", @"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._ -]{0,122}\\.(HEIC|heic|JPG|jpg|JPEG|jpeg|PNG|png)"]];
   216	  names = [names sortedArrayUsingSelector:@selector(compare:)];
   217	  NSMutableArray *files = [NSMutableArray arrayWithObject:@{@"scan": @YES, @"url": model, @"type": @"model/vnd.usdz+zip"}];
   218	  for (NSString *name in STMObjectsSpread(names, (NSUInteger)maxPhotos)) {
   219	    if ([name containsString:@".."]) continue;
   220	    NSString *ext = name.pathExtension.lowercaseString;
   221	    NSString *type = [ext isEqual:@"heic"] ? @"image/heic" : [ext isEqual:@"png"] ? @"image/png" : @"image/jpeg";
   222	    [files addObject:@{@"name": name, @"url": [[run URLByAppendingPathComponent:@"Images" isDirectory:YES] URLByAppendingPathComponent:name], @"type": type}];
   223	  }

---BASE CLIENT---
import type { ObjectsNative } from './native';
import type { StmObject } from './types';

export const PHOTOS_PER_SCAN = 24;

export type ObjectsErrorKind =
  | 'pairing'
  | 'unreachable'
  | 'conflict'
  | 'not-found'
  | 'not-set-up'
  | 'nothing-sent'
  | 'cancelled'
  | 'failed';

const MESSAGES: Record<ObjectsErrorKind, string> = {
  pairing: 'Connect to your Mac again.',
  unreachable:
    "Your Mac isn't answering. Check it's on and awake, then try again.",
  conflict: 'Wait until the current step finishes.',
  'not-found': 'This is no longer on your Mac.',
  'not-set-up':
    "Your Mac isn't ready to make things yet. Open Scan to Make on your Mac.",
  'nothing-sent': "The scan didn't save any files to send. Scan it again.",
  cancelled: 'Cancelled.',
  failed: 'Something went wrong talking to your Mac. Try again.',
};

/** One sentence the screen can show as is. */
export class ObjectsError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly kind: ObjectsErrorKind, message = MESSAGES[kind]) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ObjectsError';
  }
}

function fromNative(error: unknown): ObjectsError {
  if (error instanceof ObjectsError) return error;
  const code = (error as { code?: unknown })?.code;
  if (
    code === 'pairing-required' ||
    code === 'pairing-changed' ||
    code === 'certificate-changed' ||
    code === 'scope-denied'
  )
    return new ObjectsError('pairing');
  if (code === 'cancelled') return new ObjectsError('cancelled');
  if (
    typeof code === 'string' &&
    /offline|timeout|timed-out|unreachable|network|cannot-connect|connection/.test(
      code,
    )
  )
    return new ObjectsError('unreachable');
  const raw = (error as { message?: unknown })?.message;
  const message = typeof raw === 'string' && raw ? raw : undefined;
  if (code === 'busy' || code === 'conflict')
    return new ObjectsError('conflict', message);
  if (code === 'not-found') return new ObjectsError('not-found');
  if (code === 'no-model') return new ObjectsError('nothing-sent', message);
  return new ObjectsError('failed', message);
}

export class ObjectsClient {
  constructor(private readonly native: ObjectsNative) {}

  private async request<T>(
    method: 'GET' | 'POST',
    path: string,
    json?: unknown,
  ): Promise<T> {
    let reply: { status: number; body: string };
    try {
      reply = await this.native.call(
        json === undefined ? { method, path } : { method, path, json },
      );
    } catch (error) {
      throw fromNative(error);
    }
    let body: { error?: { code?: string; message?: string } } & Record<
      string,
      unknown
    > = {};
    try {
      body = reply.body ? JSON.parse(reply.body) : {};
    } catch {
      throw new ObjectsError('failed');
    }
    if (reply.status >= 200 && reply.status < 300) return body as T;
    if (reply.status === 401 || reply.status === 403)
      throw new ObjectsError('pairing');
    if (reply.status === 404)
      throw new ObjectsError(
        path === '/v1/objects' ? 'not-set-up' : 'not-found',
      );
    if (reply.status === 409)
      throw new ObjectsError('conflict', body.error?.message || undefined);
    throw new ObjectsError('failed');
  }
  private async object(method: 'GET' | 'POST', path: string, json?: unknown) {
    return (await this.request<{ object: StmObject }>(method, path, json))
      .object;
  }

  async list() {
    return (await this.request<{ objects: StmObject[] }>('GET', '/v1/objects'))
      .objects;
  }
  get(id: string) {
    return this.object('GET', `/v1/objects/${id}`);
  }
  create(instructions: string) {
    const text = instructions.trim();
    return this.object('POST', '/v1/objects', text ? { instructions } : {});
  }
  start(id: string) {
    return this.object('POST', `/v1/objects/${id}/start`);
  }
  correct(id: string, note: string) {
    return this.object('POST', `/v1/objects/${id}/corrections`, { note });
  }
  bestGuess(id: string) {
    return this.object('POST', `/v1/objects/${id}/closeup`, {
      bestGuess: true,
    });
  }
  /** After close-ups were added: run the touch-up again with them. */
  sendCloseups(id: string) {
    return this.object('POST', `/v1/objects/${id}/closeup`, {
      bestGuess: false,
    });
  }
  /** `checked`: the user looked at the printer after a print that may have started. */
  print(id: string, options: { checked?: boolean } = {}) {
    return this.object(
      'POST',
      `/v1/objects/${id}/print`,
      options.checked ? { checked: true } : undefined,
    );
  }
  plateClear(id: string) {
    return this.object('POST', `/v1/objects/${id}/plate`, { clear: true });
  }

  /** Sends the finished scan and its own photos, then starts the touch-up. */
  async sendCapture(id: string, runID: string) {
    let sent: { scanSent: boolean; photosSent: number };
    try {
      sent = await this.native.sendCapture({
        objectId: id,
        runID,
        maxPhotos: PHOTOS_PER_SCAN,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      throw fromNative(error);
    }
    if (!sent.scanSent && sent.photosSent === 0)
      throw new ObjectsError('nothing-sent');
    return this.start(id);
  }
  async pickPhotos(id: string, limit: number) {
    try {
      return (await this.native.pickPhotos({ objectId: id, limit })).photosSent;
    } catch (error) {
      throw fromNative(error);
    }
  }
  async takePhoto(id: string) {
    try {
      return (await this.native.takePhoto({ objectId: id })).photosSent;
    } catch (error) {
      throw fromNative(error);
    }
  }
  async renderUri(object: StmObject, render: string) {
    const name = `renders/${render.split('/').pop()}`;
    try {
      return (
        await this.native.download({
          objectId: object.id,
          name,
          version: object.updatedAtMs,
        })
      ).uri;
    } catch (error) {
      throw fromNative(error);
    }
  }
  /** The printer camera's photo while the plate check waits. */
  async plateUri(object: StmObject) {
    try {
      return (
        await this.native.download({
          objectId: object.id,
          name: 'plate.jpg',
          version: object.updatedAtMs,
        })
      ).uri;
    } catch (error) {
      throw fromNative(error);
    }
  }
  async showModel(object: StmObject) {
    try {
      await this.native.showModel({
        objectId: object.id,
        version: object.updatedAtMs,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      throw fromNative(error);
    }
  }
}

---BASE CAPTURE---
import {
  objectCaptureNative,
  type NativeCaptureDraft,
  type ObjectCaptureNative,
} from '../capture/nativeObjectCapture';

/**
 * The LiDAR capture for one object. The capture keeps the object's id as its
 * origin, so a scan that finished but wasn't sent (the app was closed) is
 * found again and sent.
 */
export interface ObjectCapture {
  /** True when this device can scan (LiDAR). */
  available(): Promise<boolean>;
  /** Opens the capture; resolves when it closes, with the run if a model was made. */
  scan(objectId: string): Promise<string | null>;
  /** A finished run for this object that may not have been sent yet. */
  finished(objectId: string): Promise<string | null>;
}

export function objectCapture(
  native: ObjectCaptureNative | null = objectCaptureNative(),
): ObjectCapture {
  const ready = (draft: NativeCaptureDraft | null | undefined) =>
    draft?.status === 'ready' ? draft.runID : null;
  return {
    async available() {
      if (!native) return false;
      const status = await native.status().catch(() => null);
      return !!status?.supported && status.lidar !== false;
    },
    async scan(objectId) {
      if (!native) return null;
      return ready(
        await native.start({
          projectId: objectId,
          revision: 0,
          snapshot: '{}',
          context: null,
        }),
      );
    },
    async finished(objectId) {
      if (!native) return null;
      const drafts = await native.drafts().catch(() => []);
      return ready(drafts.find(draft => draft.origin.projectId === objectId));
    },
  };
}

---BASE START---
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { Button, Field, Screen } from '../ui/components';
import { colors, space, type } from '../ui/theme';
import { ObjectsError, type ObjectsClient } from '../objects/client';
import type { ObjectCapture } from '../objects/capture';

/**
 * Starting something: what to make (optional), then a scan, photos, or
 * nothing more when the description names a product with published sizes.
 */
export function StartScreen({
  client,
  capture,
  onBack,
  onStarted,
  onPair,
}: {
  client: ObjectsClient;
  capture: ObjectCapture;
  onBack: () => void;
  onStarted: (id: string) => void;
  onPair: () => void;
}) {
  const [instructions, setInstructions] = useState('');
  const [canScan, setCanScan] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [working, setWorking] = useState<'scan' | 'photos' | 'make' | null>(
    null,
  );
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<ObjectsError | null>(null);
  const inFlight = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    capture.available().then(setCanScan, () => setCanScan(false));
  }, [capture]);

  const begin = async (kind: 'scan' | 'photos' | 'make') => {
    // A ref, not state: a double tap lands before the next render.
    if (inFlight.current) return;
    inFlight.current = true;
    setWorking(kind);
    setProblem(null);
    let id: string | null = null;
    try {
      const object = await client.create(instructions);
      id = object.id;
      if (kind === 'scan') {
        const runID = await capture.scan(object.id);
        if (runID) await client.sendCapture(object.id, runID);
      } else if (kind === 'photos') {
        if (await client.pickPhotos(object.id, 12))
          await client.start(object.id);
      } else await client.start(object.id);
      onStarted(object.id);
    } catch (e) {
      const error = e instanceof ObjectsError ? e : new ObjectsError('failed');
      // Once it exists, its own page says what's left to do.
      if (id && error.kind !== 'pairing') onStarted(id);
      else setProblem(error);
    } finally {
      inFlight.current = false;
      setWorking(null);
    }
  };
  const described = !!instructions.trim();
  return (
    <Screen title="What do you want to make?" onBack={onBack}>
      <Field
        label="Describe it"
        hint="Leave it empty to make a copy of what you scan. Product names, model numbers and links all help."
        placeholder="A clip that holds a 6 mm cable to the edge of my desk"
        value={instructions}
        onChangeText={setInstructions}
        multiline
        maxLength={8000}
        autoCorrect
      />
      <View style={styles.actions}>
        {canScan ? (
          <Button
            label={working === 'scan' ? 'Sending to your Mac…' : 'Scan it'}
            systemImage="viewfinder"
            disabled={!!working}
            onPress={() => begin('scan')}
          />
        ) : null}
        <Button
          label={working === 'photos' ? 'Sending to your Mac…' : 'Use photos'}
          variant={canScan ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
          systemImage="photo.on.rectangle"
          disabled={!!working}
          onPress={() => begin('photos')}
        />
        {described ? (
          <Button
            label={
              working === 'make'
                ? 'Sending to your Mac…'
                : 'Make it from the description'
            }
            variant="secondary"
            disabled={!!working}
            onPress={() => begin('make')}
          />
        ) : null}
      </View>
      {canScan === false ? (
        <Text style={styles.note}>
          Scanning needs an iPhone or iPad with LiDAR.
        </Text>
      ) : null}
      {problem ? (
        <View accessibilityLiveRegion="assertive" style={styles.problem}>
          <Text style={styles.problemText}>{problem.message}</Text>
          {problem.kind === 'pairing' ? (
            <Button label="Connect to your Mac" onPress={onPair} />
          ) : null}
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </Screen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  actions: { marginTop: space.xl, gap: space.md },
  note: { ...type.caption, marginTop: space.lg },
  problem: { marginTop: space.lg, gap: space.md },
  problemText: { ...type.body, color: colors.fail },
});

---CAPTURE SWIFT---
@MainActor @objc(NativeCaptureCoordinator)
final class NativeCaptureCoordinator: NSObject {
    // A bridge reload destroys its module, never the active session owner.
    static let owner = NativeCaptureRunOwner(store: NativeCaptureDraftStore()) { GuidedNativeCaptureEngine(directory: $0) }
    private var host: UIViewController?
    private var completion: RCTPromiseResolveBlock?
    private var rejection: RCTPromiseRejectBlock?
    private var presentedRunID: String?
    @objc nonisolated static func requiresMainQueueSetup() -> Bool { true }
    @objc nonisolated func methodQueue() -> DispatchQueue { .main }
    @objc func status(_ resolve: RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: RCTPromiseRejectBlock) {
        // Scene depth is only available with a LiDAR scanner; it lets the app say why scanning is off.
        resolve(["supported": ObjectCaptureSession.isSupported && PhotogrammetrySession.isSupported,
                 "lidar": ARWorldTrackingConfiguration.supportsFrameSemantics(.sceneDepth),
                 "activeRunID": Self.owner.activeRunID as Any? ?? NSNull()])
    }
    @objc func drafts(_ resolve: RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: RCTPromiseRejectBlock) {
        do { resolve(try Self.owner.store.list(activeRunID: Self.owner.activeRunID).map { try $0.dictionary() }) }
        catch { reject("storage-error", error.localizedDescription, error) }
    }
    @objc func start(_ options: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock,
                     reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock) {
        guard ObjectCaptureSession.isSupported && PhotogrammetrySession.isSupported else {
            reject("unsupported", "Scanning is unavailable on this device. Choose a USDZ file instead.", nil); return
        }
        guard host == nil, let presenter = RCTPresentedViewController(), presenter.presentedViewController == nil else {
            reject("busy", "Close the current sheet first.", nil); return
        }
        do {
            let origin = try JSONDecoder().decode(NativeCaptureDestination.self,
                from: JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: options))
            let draft = try Self.owner.start(origin: origin)
            guard let engine = Self.owner.engine as? GuidedNativeCaptureEngine else {
                // Synchronous startup failure was retained by the owner.
                resolve(try Self.owner.store.read(draft.runID).dictionary()); return
            }
            completion = resolve; rejection = reject; presentedRunID = draft.runID
            let controller = UIHostingController(rootView: NativeCaptureHostView(owner: Self.owner, model: engine.model) { [weak self] add in self?.close(add: add) })
            controller.modalPresentationStyle = .fullScreen; controller.isModalInPresentation = true
            host = controller; presenter.present(controller, animated: true)
        } catch { reject("capture-error", error.localizedDescription, error) }
    }
    private func close(add: Bool) {
        guard let id = presentedRunID else { return }
        Self.owner.stop()
        let resolve = completion; let reject = rejection
        completion = nil; rejection = nil; presentedRunID = nil
        let controller = host; host = nil
        controller?.dismiss(animated: true) {
            do { var result = try Self.owner.store.read(id).dictionary(); result["addRequested"] = add; resolve?(result) }
            catch { reject?("storage-error", error.localizedDescription, error) }
        }
    }
    @objc func stop(_ resolve: RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: RCTPromiseRejectBlock) { Self.owner.stop(); resolve(nil) }
    @objc nonisolated func invalidate() {
        Task { @MainActor [self] in
            // Invalidation is an interruption; no implicit restart when the bridge comes back.
            if let id = presentedRunID, Self.owner.activeRunID == id { Self.owner.stop() }
            completion = nil; rejection = nil; presentedRunID = nil
            host?.dismiss(animated: false); host = nil
        }
    }
}
#endif

---SERVER OBJECT CONTRACT---
services/companion/src/objects/store.ts:5:export type ObjectState = 'new' | 'touching-up' | 'needs-closeup' | 'ready' | 'printing' | 'printed' | 'failed';

---QUEUE PROVIDER---
cat: apps/mobile/src/screens/KeepPairingFresh.tsx: No such file or directory
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

/**
 * The companion bridge serves one request at a time and rejects overlap.
 * Every screen sends its companion calls through one queue, so a request
 * left running by a screen the user has just left can't collide with the
 * next screen's first call. Never call the gate from inside a gated task.
 */
export type RequestGate = <T>(task: () => Promise<T>) => Promise<T>;

export function createRequestGate(): RequestGate {
  let tail: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
  return task => {
    const run = tail.then(task);
    tail = run.then(
      () => undefined,
      () => undefined,
    );
    return run;
  };
}

const SharedGate = createContext<RequestGate | null>(null);

/** One queue for the whole app. */
export function RequestGateProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [gate] = useState(createRequestGate);
  return <SharedGate.Provider value={gate}>{children}</SharedGate.Provider>;
}

/** The app's shared queue, or a queue of the screen's own outside the app. */
export function useRequestGate(): RequestGate {
  const shared = useContext(SharedGate);
  const [own] = useState(createRequestGate);
  return shared ?? own;
}

  return {
    async handle(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse, authorize: (scope: RemoteScope) => Promise<void>, _header: (name: string) => string | undefined): Promise<void> {
      const url = new URL(request.url ?? '/', 'http://localhost');
      const match = PATH.exec(url.pathname);
      try {
        if (!match) throw new HttpFailure(404, 'not-found');
        const [, id, action] = match;
        const method = request.method ?? 'GET';
        if (!id) {
          if (method === 'GET') { await authorize(READ); return send(response, 200, {objects: (await service.list()).map(publicObject)}); }
          if (method === 'POST') {
            await authorize(WRITE);
            const {instructions} = await json<{instructions?: unknown}>(request);
            if (instructions !== undefined && (typeof instructions !== 'string' || instructions.length > 8000)) throw new HttpFailure(400, 'invalid-request');
            return send(response, 201, {object: publicObject(await service.create(instructions))});
          }
          throw new HttpFailure(405, 'method-not-allowed');
        }
        if (!action) {
          if (method === 'DELETE') {
            await authorize(WRITE);
            await service.delete(id);
            return send(response, 200, {deleted: true});
          }
          if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpFailure(405, 'method-not-allowed');
          await authorize(READ);
          return send(response, 200, {object: publicObject(await service.get(id))});
        }
        if (action === 'plate.jpg') {
          if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpFailure(405, 'method-not-allowed');
          await authorize(PRINT);
          if (!(await service.get(id)).plate?.photo) throw new HttpFailure(404, 'not-found');
          const bytes = await readFile(join(service.dir(id), 'plate.jpg')).catch(() => { throw new HttpFailure(404, 'not-found'); });
          response.statusCode = 200;
          response.setHeader('content-type', 'image/jpeg');
          response.end(bytes);
          return;
        }
        if (action === 'model.stl' || action.startsWith('renders/')) {
          if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpFailure(405, 'method-not-allowed');

- (void)retire {
  [self.task cancel]; self.task = nil; [self.session invalidateAndCancel]; self.session = nil;
}
- (void)clear {
  [self retire];
  self.bytes = nil; self.pending = nil; self.snapshot = nil; self.resolve = nil; self.reject = nil;
  self.remaining = nil; self.active = nil; self.failure = nil; self.pairCode = nil; self.pairName = nil;
}
- (void)fail:(NSString *)code {
  RCTPromiseRejectBlock reject = self.reject; [self clear];
  if (reject) reject(code, @"Companion connection could not complete.", nil);
}

}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(check, checkResolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  if (self.task) { reject(@"busy", @"A connection check is already running.", nil); return; }
  NSError *error = nil; NSDictionary *r = STMReadPairing(&error);
  if (error || !r || [r[@"expiresAt"] doubleValue] <= NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970*1000) { reject(error ? @"secure-storage" : @"pairing-required", @"Connect this device to a companion.", nil); return; }
  self.pairing = NO; self.refreshing = NO; self.snapshot = r; self.resolve = resolve; self.reject = reject;
  self.remaining = STMAttemptOrder(r); self.active = r;
  [self next];
}
// Starts the request for the next address: pairing at it, or checking the stored credential there.
- (void)next {
  NSString *address = self.remaining.firstObject;
  self.remaining = [self.remaining subarrayWithRange:NSMakeRange(1, self.remaining.count - 1)];
  NSMutableDictionary *record = [self.active mutableCopy];
  // Pairing: the answering address becomes baseUrl. Checking: only activeUrl moves, so the
  // companion keeps the same baseUrl (and local storage) whichever address answers.
  if (self.pairing) record[@"baseUrl"] = address;
  else if ([address isEqual:record[@"baseUrl"]]) [record removeObjectForKey:@"activeUrl"];
  else record[@"activeUrl"] = address;
  NSURL *base = STMPairingBaseURL(record);
  if (self.pairing) self.pending = record; else self.active = record;
  NSMutableURLRequest *request;
  if (self.pairing) {
    request = [NSMutableURLRequest requestWithURL:[base URLByAppendingPathComponent:@"v1/pair"]];
    request.HTTPMethod = @"POST";
    request.HTTPBody = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:@{@"code":self.pairCode,@"deviceName":self.pairName} options:0 error:nil];
    [request setValue:@"application/json" forHTTPHeaderField:@"Content-Type"];
  } else {
    request = [NSMutableURLRequest requestWithURL:[base URLByAppendingPathComponent:@"healthz"]];
    [request setValue:[@"Bearer " stringByAppendingString:record[@"token"]] forHTTPHeaderField:@"Authorization"];
    [request setValue:record[@"deviceId"] forHTTPHeaderField:@"X-Scan-Device"];
  }
  [self start:request resolve:self.resolve reject:self.reject];
}
// Rolling refresh (POST /v1/auth/refresh, companion #147): the same token,
// valid for another full lifetime. No body; the token never reaches JS.
RCT_REMAP_METHOD(refresh, refreshResolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  if (self.task) { reject(@"busy", @"A connection check is already running.", nil); return; }
  NSError *error = nil; NSDictionary *r = STMReadPairing(&error);
  if (error || !r || [r[@"expiresAt"] doubleValue] <= NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970*1000) { reject(error ? @"secure-storage" : @"pairing-required", @"Connect this device to a companion.", nil); return; }
  self.pairing = NO; self.refreshing = YES; self.snapshot = r;
  NSMutableURLRequest *request = [NSMutableURLRequest requestWithURL:[STMPairingBaseURL(r) URLByAppendingPathComponent:@"v1/auth/refresh"]];
  request.HTTPMethod = @"POST"; request.HTTPBody = [NSData data];
  [request setValue:[@"Bearer " stringByAppendingString:r[@"token"]] forHTTPHeaderField:@"Authorization"];
  [request setValue:r[@"deviceId"] forHTTPHeaderField:@"X-Scan-Device"];
  [self start:request resolve:resolve reject:reject];
}
- (void)URLSession:(NSURLSession *)session didReceiveChallenge:(NSURLAuthenticationChallenge *)challenge completionHandler:(void (^)(NSURLSessionAuthChallengeDisposition, NSURLCredential *))completionHandler {
  if (STMPinnedTrust(challenge, self.pairing ? self.pending : (self.active ?: self.snapshot), completionHandler)) self.pinRejected = YES;
}
- (void)URLSession:(NSURLSession *)session task:(NSURLSessionTask *)task willPerformHTTPRedirection:(NSHTTPURLResponse *)response newRequest:(NSURLRequest *)request completionHandler:(void (^)(NSURLRequest *))completionHandler { completionHandler(nil); }
    // An unreachable address beats another server's certificate as the reason to report.
    if (!self.failure || [@[@"network", @"timeout"] containsObject:failure]) self.failure = failure;
    if (self.remaining.count && STMTryNextAddress(failure)) { [self retire]; [self next]; return; }
    [self fail:self.remaining.count ? failure : self.failure]; return;
  }
  if (self.status != 200) {
    NSString *code = self.status == 401 ? @"unauthorized" : self.status == 403 ? @"forbidden" : self.status == 429 ? @"rate-limited" : self.status >= 300 && self.status < 400 ? @"wrong-host" : @"unavailable";
    [self fail:code]; return;
  }
  id json = [NSJSONSerialization JSONObjectWithData:self.bytes options:0 error:nil];
  if (![json isKindOfClass:NSDictionary.class]) { [self fail:@"invalid-response"]; return; }
  NSDictionary *result;
  if (self.pairing) {
    NSMutableDictionary *r = [json mutableCopy];
    // The address, pin and address list come from the invitation, never the response.
    [r removeObjectsForKeys:@[@"certificateSha256", @"publicKeySha256", @"addresses", @"activeUrl"]];
    for (NSString *key in @[@"baseUrl", @"certificateSha256", @"publicKeySha256", @"addresses"])
      if (self.pending[key]) r[key] = self.pending[key];
    if (!ValidRecord(r) || [r[@"expiresAt"] doubleValue] <= NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970*1000 || [r[@"expiresAt"] doubleValue] > NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970*1000+31.0*86400000) { [self fail:@"invalid-response"]; return; }
    NSData *data = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:r options:0 error:nil];
    NSDictionary *attrs = @{(__bridge id)kSecValueData:data, (__bridge id)kSecAttrAccessible:(__bridge id)kSecAttrAccessibleWhenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly};
    OSStatus status = SecItemUpdate((__bridge CFDictionaryRef)Query(), (__bridge CFDictionaryRef)attrs);
    if (status == errSecItemNotFound) { NSMutableDictionary *q = [Query() mutableCopy]; [q addEntriesFromDictionary:attrs]; status = SecItemAdd((__bridge CFDictionaryRef)q, NULL); }
    if (status != errSecSuccess) { [self fail:@"secure-storage"]; return; }
      [[NSNotificationCenter defaultCenter] postNotificationName:STMPairingChanged object:nil];
    result = STMPairingMetadata(r);
  } else if (self.refreshing) {
    NSError *readError = nil; NSDictionary *current = STMReadPairing(&readError);
    if (readError || ![current isEqual:self.snapshot]) { [self fail:@"pairing-changed"]; return; }
    // The same credential back, with a later expiry no more than one
    // lifetime away; only the expiry and scopes are taken from it.
    double now = NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970*1000;
    NSMutableDictionary *r = [current mutableCopy]; r[@"expiresAt"] = json[@"expiresAt"]; r[@"scopes"] = json[@"scopes"];
    if (![json[@"protocolVersion"] isEqual:current[@"protocolVersion"]] || ![json[@"deviceId"] isEqual:current[@"deviceId"]] ||
        ![json[@"token"] isEqual:current[@"token"]] || !ValidRecord(r) ||
        [r[@"expiresAt"] doubleValue] <= now || [r[@"expiresAt"] doubleValue] > now+31.0*86400000) { [self fail:@"invalid-response"]; return; }
    NSData *data = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:r options:0 error:nil];
    NSDictionary *attrs = @{(__bridge id)kSecValueData:data, (__bridge id)kSecAttrAccessible:(__bridge id)kSecAttrAccessibleWhenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly};
    OSStatus status = SecItemUpdate((__bridge CFDictionaryRef)Query(), (__bridge CFDictionaryRef)attrs);
    if (status == errSecItemNotFound) { [self fail:@"pairing-changed"]; return; }
    if (status != errSecSuccess) { [self fail:@"secure-storage"]; return; }
    // Still the same pairing (STMSamePairing), so no STMPairingChanged:
    // uploads and checks in flight carry on.
    result = STMPairingMetadata(r);
  } else {
    NSError *readError = nil; NSDictionary *current = STMReadPairing(&readError);
    if (readError || ![current isEqual:self.snapshot]) { [self fail:@"pairing-changed"]; return; }
    if (![json[@"protocolVersion"] isEqual:@"0.1.0"]) { [self fail:@"invalid-response"]; return; }
    // Another address answered: keep it first for this and every later request.
    if (![self.active isEqual:current]) {
      NSData *data = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:self.active options:0 error:nil];
      NSDictionary *attrs = @{(__bridge id)kSecValueData:data, (__bridge id)kSecAttrAccessible:(__bridge id)kSecAttrAccessibleWhenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly};
      if (!ValidRecord(self.active) || SecItemUpdate((__bridge CFDictionaryRef)Query(), (__bridge CFDictionaryRef)attrs) != errSecSuccess) { [self fail:@"secure-storage"]; return; }
      // Another of the Mac's addresses is still the same pairing (STMSamePairing): no STMPairingChanged.
      current = self.active;
    }
    result = STMPairingMetadata(current);
  }
  RCTPromiseResolveBlock resolve = self.resolve; [self clear]; resolve(result);
}

@interface ObjectsBridge : NSObject <RCTBridgeModule, PHPickerViewControllerDelegate, UIImagePickerControllerDelegate, UINavigationControllerDelegate, UIAdaptivePresentationControllerDelegate>
@property NSMutableSet<NSString *> *uploading;
@property NSString *pickObject;
@property(copy) RCTPromiseResolveBlock pickResolve;
@property(copy) RCTPromiseRejectBlock pickReject;
@end

@implementation ObjectsBridge
RCT_EXPORT_MODULE()
+ (BOOL)requiresMainQueueSetup { return NO; }
- (dispatch_queue_t)methodQueue { return dispatch_get_main_queue(); }
- (instancetype)init { if ((self = [super init])) _uploading = [NSMutableSet set]; return self; }

static NSString *STMObjectsMessage(NSString *code) {
  NSDictionary *messages = @{
    @"pairing-required": @"Connect to your Mac again.", @"scope-denied": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
    @"pairing-changed": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
    @"certificate-changed": @"Your Mac presented a different certificate. Scan a new QR code from it.",
    @"unreachable": @"Your Mac isn't answering. Check it's on and awake, then try again.",
    @"payload-too-large": @"That file is too large to send.",
    @"invalid-request": @"That request isn't allowed.",
  };
  return messages[code] ?: @"Something went wrong talking to your Mac. Try again.";
}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(call, callOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  NSString *method = o[@"method"], *path = o[@"path"], *scope = STMObjectsPath(path, method), *code = nil;
  id json = o[@"json"];
  if (!scope || ([method isEqual:@"GET"] && json)) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  NSDictionary *pairing = STMObjectsPairing(scope, &code);
  if (!pairing) { reject(code, STMObjectsMessage(code), nil); return; }
  NSData *body = nil;
  if (json) {
    if (![NSJSONSerialization isValidJSONObject:json]) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
    body = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:json options:0 error:nil];
    if (!body || body.length > 16 * 1024) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  }
  [STMObjectsRequest send:method path:path body:body type:@"application/json" pairing:pairing limit:STMObjectsJSONLimit done:^(NSInteger status, NSData *data, NSString *failure) {
    if (failure) { reject(failure, STMObjectsMessage(failure), nil); return; }
    NSString *text = data.length ? [[NSString alloc] initWithData:data encoding:NSUTF8StringEncoding] : @"";
    if (!text) { reject(@"invalid-response", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-response"), nil); return; }
    resolve(@{@"status": @(status), @"body": text});
  }];
}

/** Sends files one after another; stops at the first failure. */

- (BOOL)sheetBusy:(UIViewController *)presenter { return self.pickResolve || !presenter || presenter.presentedViewController; }
- (void)finishPick:(NSArray<NSDictionary *> *)photos {
  // Chosen on the start screen: kept here until Make it.
  if (self.pickResolve && !self.pickObject) { [self finishChosen:photos]; return; }
  RCTPromiseResolveBlock resolve = self.pickResolve; RCTPromiseRejectBlock reject = self.pickReject; NSString *objectId = self.pickObject;
  self.pickResolve = nil; self.pickReject = nil; self.pickObject = nil;
  if (!resolve) return;
  if (!photos.count) { resolve(@{@"photosSent": @0}); return; }
  [self sendFiles:photos objectId:objectId resolve:^{ resolve(@{@"photosSent": @(photos.count)}); } reject:reject];
}
static NSDictionary *STMObjectsPhoto(NSData *data, NSString *ext) {
  NSString *type = [ext isEqual:@"png"] ? @"image/png" : [ext isEqual:@"heic"] ? @"image/heic" : @"image/jpeg";
  NSString *name = [NSString stringWithFormat:@"closeup-%lld-%@.%@", (long long)(NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970 * 1000), [NSUUID.UUID.UUIDString substringToIndex:8].lowercaseString, ext];
  return @{@"name": name, @"bytes": data, @"type": type};
}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(pickPhotos, pickPhotosOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  NSInteger limit = [o[@"limit"] integerValue];
  UIViewController *presenter = RCTPresentedViewController();
  if (!STMObjectsMatches(o[@"objectId"], STMObjectsUUID) || limit < 1 || limit > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  if ([self sheetBusy:presenter]) { reject(@"busy", @"Close the current sheet first.", nil); return; }
  PHPickerConfiguration *configuration = [[PHPickerConfiguration alloc] init];
  configuration.filter = [PHPickerFilter imagesFilter]; configuration.selectionLimit = limit;
  configuration.preferredAssetRepresentationMode = PHPickerConfigurationAssetRepresentationModeCurrent;
  PHPickerViewController *picker = [[PHPickerViewController alloc] initWithConfiguration:configuration];
  picker.delegate = self; picker.presentationController.delegate = self;
  self.pickObject = o[@"objectId"]; self.pickResolve = resolve; self.pickReject = reject;
  [presenter presentViewController:picker animated:YES completion:nil];
}
- (void)presentationControllerDidDismiss:(UIPresentationController *)controller { [self finishPick:@[]]; }
- (void)picker:(PHPickerViewController *)picker didFinishPicking:(NSArray<PHPickerResult *> *)results {
  [picker dismissViewControllerAnimated:YES completion:nil];
  if (!self.pickResolve) return;
  NSMutableArray *photos = [NSMutableArray array];
  dispatch_group_t group = dispatch_group_create();
  for (PHPickerResult *result in results) {
    NSString *identifier = nil, *ext = nil;
    for (NSArray *pair in @[@[UTTypeHEIC, @"heic"], @[UTTypeJPEG, @"jpg"], @[UTTypePNG, @"png"]])
      if (!identifier && [result.itemProvider hasItemConformingToTypeIdentifier:[pair[0] identifier]]) { identifier = [pair[0] identifier]; ext = pair[1]; }
    if (!identifier) continue;
    dispatch_group_enter(group);
    [result.itemProvider loadDataRepresentationForTypeIdentifier:identifier completionHandler:^(NSData *data, NSError *error) {
      dispatch_async(dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
        if (data.length && data.length <= STMObjectsPhotoLimit) [photos addObject:STMObjectsPhoto(data, ext)];
        dispatch_group_leave(group);
      });
    }];
  }
  dispatch_group_notify(group, dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{ [self finishPick:photos]; });
}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(takePhoto, takePhotoOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  UIViewController *presenter = RCTPresentedViewController();
  if (!STMObjectsMatches(o[@"objectId"], STMObjectsUUID)) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  if (![UIImagePickerController isSourceTypeAvailable:UIImagePickerControllerSourceTypeCamera]) { reject(@"no-camera", @"This device has no camera. Attach photos instead.", nil); return; }
  if ([self sheetBusy:presenter]) { reject(@"busy", @"Close the current sheet first.", nil); return; }
  UIImagePickerController *camera = [UIImagePickerController new];
  camera.sourceType = UIImagePickerControllerSourceTypeCamera; camera.delegate = self; camera.presentationController.delegate = self;
  self.pickObject = o[@"objectId"]; self.pickResolve = resolve; self.pickReject = reject;
  [presenter presentViewController:camera animated:YES completion:nil];
}
- (void)imagePickerController:(UIImagePickerController *)picker didFinishPickingMediaWithInfo:(NSDictionary<UIImagePickerControllerInfoKey, id> *)info {
  [picker dismissViewControllerAnimated:YES completion:nil];
  NSData *jpeg = UIImageJPEGRepresentation(info[UIImagePickerControllerOriginalImage], 0.9);
  [self finishPick:jpeg.length ? @[STMObjectsPhoto(jpeg, @"jpg")] : @[]];
}
- (void)imagePickerControllerDidCancel:(UIImagePickerController *)picker {
  [picker dismissViewControllerAnimated:YES completion:nil];
  [self finishPick:@[]];
}

---REFRESH + BASE DETAIL---
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { AppState } from 'react-native';
import { useRequestGate, type RequestGate } from '../screens/requestGate';
import { companionNative, type CompanionNative } from './pairing';

const DAY = 86_400_000;
/**
 * A paired credential lasts 30 days. Under 20 days left, the phone asks your
 * Mac for another 30 days of the same credential, so at most about every 10
 * days (the Mac also turns away refreshes that come too often).
 */
export const REFRESH_WITHIN = 20 * DAY;
// Your Mac turned the credential away: this phone has to pair again.
const REFUSED = new Set(['unauthorized', 'forbidden', 'pairing-required']);

export type Freshness = 'fresh' | 'refreshed' | 'skipped' | 'pair-again';

/**
 * Keeps the paired Mac's credential alive (KAN-182). Quiet unless your Mac
 * refuses it; a Mac that can't be reached is simply asked again next time.
 * The request waits its turn in the app's one queue of calls to your Mac.
 */
export async function keepPairingFresh(
  native: CompanionNative,
  gate: RequestGate,
  now: () => number = Date.now,
): Promise<Freshness> {
  let status;
  try {
    status = await native.status();
  } catch {
    return 'skipped';
  }
  // Too late to refresh: your Mac accepts only a live credential.
  if (status.state === 'expired') return 'pair-again';
  if (status.state !== 'paired' || typeof status.expiresAt !== 'number')
    return 'skipped';
  if (status.expiresAt - now() > REFRESH_WITHIN) return 'fresh';
  const refresh = native.refresh;
  if (!refresh) return 'skipped';
  try {
    await gate(() => refresh());
    return 'refreshed';
  } catch (error) {
    const code = (error as { code?: string })?.code ?? '';
    return REFUSED.has(code) ? 'pair-again' : 'skipped';
  }
}

/**
 * Runs keepPairingFresh when the app opens and each time it comes back to
 * the front. Renders nothing; tells the app when the pairing needs a look.
 */
export function KeepPairingFresh({
  native = companionNative(),
  onPairAgain,
}: {
  native?: CompanionNative;
  /** Your Mac refused the credential: screens should read the pairing again. */
  onPairAgain: () => void;
}) {
  const gate = useRequestGate();
  const told = useRef(onPairAgain);
  told.current = onPairAgain;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!native) return;
    let running = false;
    let live = true;
    const run = () => {
      if (running) return;
      running = true;
      keepPairingFresh(native, gate)
        .then(result => {
          if (live && result === 'pair-again') told.current();
        })
        .finally(() => {
          running = false;
        });
    };
    run();
    const subscription = AppState.addEventListener('change', state => {
      if (state === 'active') run();
    });
    return () => {
      live = false;
      subscription.remove();
    };
  }, [native, gate]);
  return null;
}
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AppState,
  Image,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { Button, Field, Screen } from '../ui/components';

  describeObject,
  formatSize,
  type ObjectAction,
} from '../objects/model';

const POLL_MS = 3000;
const TONE = {
  pending: colors.pending,
  pass: colors.pass,
  fail: colors.fail,
  accent: colors.accent,
  neutral: colors.muted,
};

/** Keeps one object up to date: polls while it is busy and the app is in front. */
export function useObject(client: ObjectsClient, id: string, refreshKey = 0) {
  const [object, setObject] = useState<StmObject | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<ObjectsError | null>(null);
  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setObject(await client.get(id));
      setError(null);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof ObjectsError ? e : new ObjectsError('failed'));
    }
  }, [client, id]);
  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load, refreshKey]);
  const busy = object ? describeObject(object).busy : false;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!busy) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (AppState.currentState !== 'background') load();
    }, POLL_MS);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [busy, load]);
  return { object, setObject, error, load };
}

function Renders({
  client,
  object,
}: {
  client: ObjectsClient;
  object: StmObject;
}) {
  const [uris, setUris] = useState<string[]>([]);
  // The space this page actually has (one pane of two on a wide screen).
  const [size, setSize] = useState(0);
  const renders = object.current?.renders ?? [];
  const key = `${object.id}:${object.updatedAtMs}:${renders.join()}`;
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    Promise.all(
      renders.map(render => client.renderUri(object, render).catch(() => null)),
    ).then(found => live && setUris(found.filter((u): u is string => !!u)));
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
    // The key covers the object version and its renders.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [key]);
  return (
    <View
      testID="render-plate"
      onLayout={event =>
        setSize(Math.round(Math.min(event.nativeEvent.layout.width, 560)))
      }
      style={[styles.plate, size ? { height: size } : styles.plateUnmeasured]}
    >
      {uris.length && size ? (
        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          pagingEnabled
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          accessibilityLabel={`Pictures of the model, ${uris.length}`}
        >
          {uris.map(uri => (
            <Image
              key={uri}
              source={{ uri }}
              resizeMode="contain"
              accessibilityIgnoresInvertColors
              testID="render-image"
              style={{ width: size, height: size }}
            />
          ))}
        </ScrollView>
      ) : (
        <Text style={styles.plateText}>
          {object.state === 'touching-up'
            ? 'The model shows here when it’s ready.'
            : 'No pictures yet.'}
        </Text>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

function PlatePhoto({
  client,
  object,
}: {
  client: ObjectsClient;
  object: StmObject;
}) {
  const [uri, setUri] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    if (object.plate?.photo)
      client.plateUri(object).then(
        found => live && setUri(found),
        () => {},
      );
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
    // A new photo comes with a new object version.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [object.id, object.updatedAtMs, object.plate?.photo]);
  if (!uri) return null;
  return (
    <Image
      source={{ uri }}
      resizeMode="cover"
      accessibilityLabel="The printer camera’s view of the plate"
      style={styles.platePhoto}
    />
  );
}

export function ObjectScreen({
  client,
  capture,
  id,
  onBack,
  onPair,
  onChanged,
  refreshKey = 0,
}: {
  client: ObjectsClient;
  capture: ObjectCapture;
  id: string;
  /** Called after an action changes the object, so the list can follow. */
  onChanged?: () => void;
  /** Bumped by the app after pairing again, to load afresh. */
  refreshKey?: number;
  /** Absent in the two-pane layout, where the list stays on screen. */
  onBack?: () => void;
  onPair: () => void;
}) {
  const { object, setObject, error, load } = useObject(client, id, refreshKey);
  const [working, setWorking] = useState<ObjectAction | 'turn' | null>(null);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<ObjectsError | null>(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
  const [canScan, setCanScan] = useState(false);
  const resumed = useRef(false);
  const inFlight = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    capture.available().then(setCanScan, () => {});
  }, [capture]);

  const run = async (
    action: ObjectAction | 'turn',
    work: () => Promise<StmObject | null | void>,
  ) => {
    // A ref, not state: a double tap lands before the next render.
    if (inFlight.current) return;
    inFlight.current = true;
    setWorking(action);
    setProblem(null);
    try {
      const next = await work();
      if (next) setObject(next);
      else await load();
      onChanged?.();
    } catch (e) {
      setProblem(e instanceof ObjectsError ? e : new ObjectsError('failed'));
    } finally {
      inFlight.current = false;
      setWorking(null);
    }
  };

  // A scan that finished but wasn't sent (the app was closed) goes now.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!object || resumed.current) return;
    // Any new object: the bridge sends only what the Mac doesn't have yet.
    if (object.state !== 'new') return;
    resumed.current = true;
    capture.finished(object.id).then(runID => {
      if (runID) run('scan', () => client.sendCapture(object.id, runID));
    });
    // Only once per object page.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [object?.id, object?.state]);

  if (!object) {
    return (
      <Screen
        title={error ? 'Can’t reach your Mac' : 'Opening…'}
        onBack={onBack}
      >
        {error ? (
          <>
            <Text style={type.body}>{error.message}</Text>
            {error.kind === 'pairing' ? (
              <Button label="Connect to your Mac" onPress={onPair} />
            ) : (

---HTTP / STORE TYPES---
import type {IncomingMessage, ServerResponse} from 'node:http';
import {readFile} from 'node:fs/promises';
import {basename, join} from 'node:path';
import type {RemoteScope} from '../remote-auth.js';
import {InvalidStateError, type ObjectService} from './service.js';
import {ObjectNotFoundError, type StmObject} from './store.js';

const READ: RemoteScope = 'projects:read', WRITE: RemoteScope = 'captures:import', PRINT: RemoteScope = 'printer-handoff:use';
const MAX_SCAN = 64 * 1024 * 1024, MAX_PHOTO = 25 * 1024 * 1024, MAX_JSON = 16 * 1024;
const PATH = /^\/v1\/objects(?:\/([0-9a-f-]{36})(?:\/(scan|photos|start|corrections|closeup|print|plate\.jpg|plate|model\.stl|renders\/[^/]+))?)?$/;

class HttpFailure extends Error {
  constructor(readonly status: number, readonly code: string, message = code) { super(message); }
}

async function body(request: IncomingMessage, limit: number): Promise<Buffer> {
  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
  let size = 0;
  for await (const chunk of request) {
    size += (chunk as Buffer).length;
    if (size > limit) throw new HttpFailure(413, 'too-large');
    chunks.push(chunk as Buffer);
  }
  return Buffer.concat(chunks);
}
async function json<T>(request: IncomingMessage): Promise<T> {
  const bytes = await body(request, MAX_JSON);
  if (!bytes.length) return {} as T;
  try { return JSON.parse(bytes.toString('utf8')) as T; } catch { throw new HttpFailure(400, 'invalid-request'); }
}

/** What the phone sees: no Mac file paths. */
export function publicObject(object: StmObject): StmObject {
  return {...object, passes: object.passes.map(({dir: _dir, ...pass}) => pass)};
}

function send(response: ServerResponse, status: number, value: unknown): void {
  response.statusCode = status;
  response.setHeader('content-type', 'application/json');
  response.end(JSON.stringify(value));
}

/** Phone-facing objects: start something, send its scan and photos, follow it, correct it, print it. */
export function createObjectRoutes(service: ObjectService) {
  return {

      const url = new URL(request.url ?? '/', 'http://localhost');
      const match = PATH.exec(url.pathname);
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {mkdir, readdir, readFile, rename, rm, writeFile} from 'node:fs/promises';
import {join} from 'node:path';

export type ObjectState = 'new' | 'touching-up' | 'needs-closeup' | 'ready' | 'printing' | 'printed' | 'failed';

export interface ObjectPass {
  startedAtMs: number;
  endedAtMs?: number;
  note?: string;
  status?: string;
  summary?: string;
  seconds?: number;
  costUsd?: number;
  dir?: string;
}

/** One thing the user scanned or asked to have made, as the phone and the Mac app both show it. */
export interface StmObject {
  id: string;
  createdAtMs: number;
  updatedAtMs: number;
  instructions?: string;
  state: ObjectState;
  /** File name under scan/. */
  scan?: string;
  /** File names under photos/, in upload order. */
  photos: string[];
  passes: ObjectPass[];
  current?: {model: string; renders: string[]; sizeMm?: [number, number, number]; summary?: string};
  question?: {closeup: string};
  /** Set while a print waits for the user: something is on the plate, or the printer has no camera. */
  plate?: {item?: string; noCamera?: boolean; photo?: boolean};
  failure?: string;
  /** The last print; `uncertain` when it may have reached the printer and must not be sent again by itself. */
  print?: {bundleId: string; uncertain?: boolean};
}

const ID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/;
const FILE_NAME = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._ -]{0,127}$/;

export class ObjectNotFoundError extends Error {
  constructor() { super('Object not found'); this.name = 'ObjectNotFoundError'; }
}

/** Objects on this Mac, one folder each: object.json plus scan/, photos/, current/ and passes/. */
export class ObjectStore {
  readonly #root: string;
  /** Read-modify-write of one object is serialized, so simultaneous changes never overwrite each other. */
  readonly #queues = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>();
  constructor(root: string) { this.#root = root; }

  dir(id: string): string {
    if (!ID.test(id)) throw new ObjectNotFoundError();
    return join(this.#root, id);
  }

  async create(instructions?: string): Promise<StmObject> {
    const now = Date.now();
    const object: StmObject = {id: randomUUID(), createdAtMs: now, updatedAtMs: now, state: 'new', photos: [], passes: [],
      ...(instructions?.trim() ? {instructions: instructions.trim()} : {})};
    await mkdir(this.dir(object.id), {recursive: true});
    await this.#write(object);
    return object;
  }

  async get(id: string): Promise<StmObject> {
    try { return JSON.parse(await readFile(join(this.dir(id), 'object.json'), 'utf8')) as StmObject; }
    catch { throw new ObjectNotFoundError(); }
  }

  async list(): Promise<StmObject[]> {
    let names: string[];
    try { names = await readdir(this.#root); } catch { return []; }
    const objects: StmObject[] = [];
    for (const name of names) if (ID.test(name)) objects.push(...await this.get(name).then(o => [o], () => []));
    return objects.sort((a, b) => b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs);
  }

  update(id: string, change: (object: StmObject) => StmObject): Promise<StmObject> {
    const previous = this.#queues.get(id) ?? Promise.resolve();
    const next = previous.catch(() => undefined).then(async () => {
      const updated = {...change(await this.get(id)), id, updatedAtMs: Date.now()};
      await this.#write(updated);
      return updated;
    });
    this.#queues.set(id, next);
    void next.finally(() => { if (this.#queues.get(id) === next) this.#queues.delete(id); }).catch(() => undefined);
    return next;
  }

  /** Stores an uploaded file under the object; the name must be a plain file name. */
  async addFile(id: string, kind: 'scan' | 'photos', name: string, bytes: Buffer): Promise<string> {
    if (!FILE_NAME.test(name) || name.includes('..')) throw new Error('Invalid file name');
    const folder = join(this.dir(id), kind);
    await mkdir(folder, {recursive: true});
    const path = join(folder, name);
    await writeFile(path, bytes);
    return path;
  }

  /** After a restart nothing is still running: mark interrupted work failed so it can be started again. */
  /** Removes the object and every file it has. */
  async remove(id: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.get(id);
    await rm(this.dir(id), {recursive: true, force: true});
  }

  async recoverInterrupted(): Promise<void> {
    for (const object of await this.list()) {
      if (object.state === 'touching-up' || object.state === 'printing') {
        await this.update(object.id, o => ({...o, state: 'failed', failure: 'interrupted when your Mac restarted the app'}));
      }
    }
  }

  async #write(object: StmObject): Promise<void> {
    const path = join(this.dir(object.id), 'object.json'), temp = `${path}.${process.pid}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
    await writeFile(temp, JSON.stringify(object, null, 2));
    await rename(temp, path);
  }
}

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  AppState,
  FlatList,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Button } from '../ui/components';
import { colors, radius, space, type } from '../ui/theme';
import { ObjectsError, type ObjectsClient } from '../objects/client';
import { describeObject } from '../objects/model';
import type { StmObject } from '../objects/types';

const POLL_MS = 3000;
const TONE = {
  pending: colors.pending,
  pass: colors.pass,
  fail: colors.fail,
  accent: colors.accent,
  neutral: colors.muted,
};

function Thumbnail({
  client,
  object,
}: {
  client: ObjectsClient;
  object: StmObject;
}) {
  const [uri, setUri] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const render = object.current?.renders[0];
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    setUri(null);
    if (render)
      client.renderUri(object, render).then(
        found => live && setUri(found),
        () => {},
      );
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
    // A new render comes with a new object version.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [object.id, object.updatedAtMs, render]);
  return (
    <View style={styles.thumb}>
      {uri ? (
        <Image
          source={{ uri }}
          resizeMode="contain"
          style={styles.thumbImage}
        />
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}

/** Everything sent to the Mac, newest first. */
export function ObjectsScreen({
  client,
  selected,
  refreshKey = 0,
  onNew,
  onOpen,
  onPair,
}: {
  client: ObjectsClient;
  /** Highlighted in the two-pane layout. */
  selected?: string | null;
  /** Bumped by the app when something changed elsewhere. */
  refreshKey?: number;
  onNew: () => void;
  onOpen: (id: string) => void;
  onPair: () => void;
}) {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [objects, setObjects] = useState<StmObject[] | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<ObjectsError | null>(null);
  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setObjects(await client.list());
      setError(null);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof ObjectsError ? e : new ObjectsError('failed'));
    }
  }, [client]);
  useEffect(() => {
    load();
  }, [load, refreshKey]);
  const busy = !!objects?.some(o => describeObject(o).busy);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!busy) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (AppState.currentState !== 'background') load();
    }, POLL_MS);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [busy, load]);

  const header = (
    <View style={styles.header}>
      <Text accessibilityRole="header" style={type.title}>
        Your things
      </Text>
      {/* A native button has no width of its own inside a row. */}
      <View style={styles.newButton}>
        <Button label="New" systemImage="plus" onPress={onNew} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
  let body: React.ReactElement | undefined;
  if (error && !objects) {
    body = (
      <View style={styles.message}>
        <Text style={type.body}>{error.message}</Text>
        {error.kind === 'pairing' ? (
          <Button label="Connect to your Mac" onPress={onPair} />
        ) : (
          <Button label="Try again" variant="secondary" onPress={load} />
        )}
      </View>
    );
  } else if (objects && !objects.length) {

      <View style={styles.message}>

---BASE NEW STATE---
import type { StmObject } from './types';

export type ObjectAction =
  | 'finishSending'
  | 'scan'
  | 'makeIt'
  | 'printChecked'
  | 'print'
  | 'correct'
  | 'openCamera'
  | 'attachPhotos'
  | 'bestGuess'
  | 'plateClear'
  | 'retry';

export interface ObjectView {
  title: string;
  status: string;
  /** Touching up or printing: the page keeps itself up to date. */
  busy: boolean;
  tone: 'pending' | 'pass' | 'fail' | 'accent' | 'neutral';
  size?: string;
  actions: ObjectAction[];
}

const WAIT = "This can take a few minutes. We'll let you know when it's ready.";

export function objectTitle(object: StmObject): string {
  const text = object.instructions?.trim();
  if (text) {
    const first = text.split(/(?<=[.!?])\s|\n/)[0].replace(/[.!?]+$/, '');
    const line = first
      .split(/,\s*(?=[a-z])/i)
      .slice(0, 2)
      .join(', ');
    if (line.length <= 44) return line;
    // Cut at a word, so the title never ends mid-word.
    const cut = line.slice(0, 43);
    return `${cut.slice(0, cut.lastIndexOf(' ')).trimEnd()}…`;
  }
  return object.scan ? 'Copy of your scan' : 'Something new';
}

export function formatSize(size: [number, number, number]): string {
  return `${size.map(n => Math.round(n)).join(' × ')} mm`;
}

export function describeObject(object: StmObject): ObjectView {
  const title = objectTitle(object);
  const size = object.current?.sizeMm
    ? `${formatSize(object.current.sizeMm)}, ready to print`
    : undefined;
  const view = (
    status: string,
    tone: ObjectView['tone'],
    actions: ObjectAction[] = [],
    busy = false,
  ): ObjectView => ({ title, status, tone, actions, busy, size });
  switch (object.state) {
    case 'new':
      // A send that stopped part way can always be finished from here.
      if (object.scan || object.photos.length)
        return view('Sending to your Mac…', 'pending', ['finishSending'], true);
      return view(
        'Waiting for a scan or photos.',
        'neutral',
        object.instructions?.trim()
          ? ['scan', 'attachPhotos', 'makeIt']
          : ['scan', 'attachPhotos'],
      );
    case 'touching-up': {
      const changing = object.passes.length > 1 && !!object.current;
      const what = changing
        ? 'Making your changes.'
        : object.scan
        ? 'Touching up your scan.'
        : 'Making it.';
      return view(`${what} ${WAIT}`, 'pending', [], true);
    }
    case 'needs-closeup':
      return view(
        `Can you send me a close-up of ${
          object.question?.closeup ?? 'the part I missed'
        }? I can't see what's happening there.`,
        'accent',
        ['openCamera', 'attachPhotos', 'bestGuess'],
      );
    case 'ready':
      return view('Ready to print', 'pass', ['print', 'correct']);
    case 'printing':
      if (object.plate?.noCamera)
        return view(
          'Check the plate is clear.',
          'accent',
          ['plateClear'],
          true,
        );
      if (object.plate)
        return view(
          `Looks like there's ${
            object.plate.item ?? 'something'
          } on your plate. Can you take it off first?`,
          'accent',
          ['plateClear'],
          true,
        );
      return view('Printing', 'pending', [], true);
    case 'printed':
      return view('Printed', 'pass', ['print', 'correct']);
    case 'failed':
      return view(
        object.failure?.trim() || 'Something went wrong on your Mac.',
        'fail',
        object.print?.uncertain
          ? ['printChecked', 'correct']
          : object.current
          ? ['print', 'correct']
          : ['retry'],
      );
  }
}
