@interface ObjectsBridge : NSObject <RCTBridgeModule, PHPickerViewControllerDelegate, UIImagePickerControllerDelegate, UINavigationControllerDelegate, UIAdaptivePresentationControllerDelegate>
@property NSMutableSet<NSString *> *uploading;
@property NSString *pickObject;
@property(copy) RCTPromiseResolveBlock pickResolve;
@property(copy) RCTPromiseRejectBlock pickReject;
@end

@implementation ObjectsBridge
RCT_EXPORT_MODULE()
+ (BOOL)requiresMainQueueSetup { return NO; }
- (dispatch_queue_t)methodQueue { return dispatch_get_main_queue(); }
- (instancetype)init { if ((self = [super init])) _uploading = [NSMutableSet set]; return self; }

static NSString *STMObjectsMessage(NSString *code) {
  NSDictionary *messages = @{
    @"pairing-required": @"Connect to your Mac again.", @"scope-denied": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
    @"pairing-changed": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
    @"certificate-changed": @"Your Mac presented a different certificate. Scan a new QR code from it.",
    @"unreachable": @"Your Mac isn't answering. Check it's on and awake, then try again.",
    @"payload-too-large": @"That file is too large to send.",
    @"invalid-request": @"That request isn't allowed.",
  };
  return messages[code] ?: @"Something went wrong talking to your Mac. Try again.";
}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(call, callOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  NSString *method = o[@"method"], *path = o[@"path"], *scope = STMObjectsPath(path, method), *code = nil;
  id json = o[@"json"];
  if (!scope || ([method isEqual:@"GET"] && json)) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  NSDictionary *pairing = STMObjectsPairing(scope, &code);
  if (!pairing) { reject(code, STMObjectsMessage(code), nil); return; }
  NSData *body = nil;
  if (json) {
    if (![NSJSONSerialization isValidJSONObject:json]) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
    body = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:json options:0 error:nil];
    if (!body || body.length > 16 * 1024) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  }
  [STMObjectsRequest send:method path:path body:body type:@"application/json" pairing:pairing limit:STMObjectsJSONLimit done:^(NSInteger status, NSData *data, NSString *failure) {
    if (failure) { reject(failure, STMObjectsMessage(failure), nil); return; }
    NSString *text = data.length ? [[NSString alloc] initWithData:data encoding:NSUTF8StringEncoding] : @"";
    if (!text) { reject(@"invalid-response", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-response"), nil); return; }

- (BOOL)sheetBusy:(UIViewController *)presenter { return self.pickResolve || !presenter || presenter.presentedViewController; }
- (void)finishPick:(NSArray<NSDictionary *> *)photos {
  // Chosen on the start screen: kept here until Make it.
  if (self.pickResolve && !self.pickObject) { [self finishChosen:photos]; return; }
  RCTPromiseResolveBlock resolve = self.pickResolve; RCTPromiseRejectBlock reject = self.pickReject; NSString *objectId = self.pickObject;
  self.pickResolve = nil; self.pickReject = nil; self.pickObject = nil;
  if (!resolve) return;
  if (!photos.count) { resolve(@{@"photosSent": @0}); return; }
  [self sendFiles:photos objectId:objectId resolve:^{ resolve(@{@"photosSent": @(photos.count)}); } reject:reject];
}
static NSDictionary *STMObjectsPhoto(NSData *data, NSString *ext) {
  NSString *type = [ext isEqual:@"png"] ? @"image/png" : [ext isEqual:@"heic"] ? @"image/heic" : @"image/jpeg";
  NSString *name = [NSString stringWithFormat:@"closeup-%lld-%@.%@", (long long)(NSDate.date.timeIntervalSince1970 * 1000), [NSUUID.UUID.UUIDString substringToIndex:8].lowercaseString, ext];
  return @{@"name": name, @"bytes": data, @"type": type};
}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(pickPhotos, pickPhotosOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  NSInteger limit = [o[@"limit"] integerValue];
  UIViewController *presenter = RCTPresentedViewController();
  if (!STMObjectsMatches(o[@"objectId"], STMObjectsUUID) || limit < 1 || limit > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  if ([self sheetBusy:presenter]) { reject(@"busy", @"Close the current sheet first.", nil); return; }
  PHPickerConfiguration *configuration = [[PHPickerConfiguration alloc] init];
  configuration.filter = [PHPickerFilter imagesFilter]; configuration.selectionLimit = limit;
  configuration.preferredAssetRepresentationMode = PHPickerConfigurationAssetRepresentationModeCurrent;
  PHPickerViewController *picker = [[PHPickerViewController alloc] initWithConfiguration:configuration];
  picker.delegate = self; picker.presentationController.delegate = self;
  self.pickObject = o[@"objectId"]; self.pickResolve = resolve; self.pickReject = reject;
  [presenter presentViewController:picker animated:YES completion:nil];
}
- (void)presentationControllerDidDismiss:(UIPresentationController *)controller { [self finishPick:@[]]; }
- (void)picker:(PHPickerViewController *)picker didFinishPicking:(NSArray<PHPickerResult *> *)results {
  [picker dismissViewControllerAnimated:YES completion:nil];
  if (!self.pickResolve) return;
  NSMutableArray *photos = [NSMutableArray array];
  dispatch_group_t group = dispatch_group_create();
  for (PHPickerResult *result in results) {
    NSString *identifier = nil, *ext = nil;
    for (NSArray *pair in @[@[UTTypeHEIC, @"heic"], @[UTTypeJPEG, @"jpg"], @[UTTypePNG, @"png"]])
      if (!identifier && [result.itemProvider hasItemConformingToTypeIdentifier:[pair[0] identifier]]) { identifier = [pair[0] identifier]; ext = pair[1]; }
    if (!identifier) continue;
    dispatch_group_enter(group);
    [result.itemProvider loadDataRepresentationForTypeIdentifier:identifier completionHandler:^(NSData *data, NSError *error) {
      dispatch_async(dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{
        if (data.length && data.length <= STMObjectsPhotoLimit) [photos addObject:STMObjectsPhoto(data, ext)];
        dispatch_group_leave(group);
      });
    }];
  }
  dispatch_group_notify(group, dispatch_get_main_queue(), ^{ [self finishPick:photos]; });
}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(takePhoto, takePhotoOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  UIViewController *presenter = RCTPresentedViewController();
  if (!STMObjectsMatches(o[@"objectId"], STMObjectsUUID)) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  if (![UIImagePickerController isSourceTypeAvailable:UIImagePickerControllerSourceTypeCamera]) { reject(@"no-camera", @"This device has no camera. Attach photos instead.", nil); return; }
  if ([self sheetBusy:presenter]) { reject(@"busy", @"Close the current sheet first.", nil); return; }
  UIImagePickerController *camera = [UIImagePickerController new];

## Subagent dispatch requires multi-agent support

Add to your Codex config (`~/.codex/config.toml`):

```toml
[features]
multi_agent = true
```

This enables the multi-agent tools that skills like
`dispatching-parallel-agents` and `subagent-driven-development` use.
Which tools you get depends on the multi-agent version your model
preset selects (current presets run V2; older ones run V1). Trust your
actual tool list over any table — including this one — when they
disagree.

- **Spawning:** give children a clean context with
  `spawn_agent {fork_turns: "none"}`; the default `"all"` copies your
  entire transcript into the child. On Codex 0.145+, role files under
  `~/.codex/agents/` attach to isolated forks via `agent_type`.
  Full-history forks accept `model` and `reasoning_effort` overrides
  (only `agent_type` is refused there) — isolated forks are the SDD
  default for context hygiene, not because overrides require them.
- **Fix rounds:** resume the implementer with `followup_task` — it
  delivers your message, triggers a turn, and transparently reloads a
  child the harness evicted. Never dispatch a fresh implementer on the
  theory that a spawned agent cannot be messaged again; on V2 it
  always can.
- **Lifecycle:** V2 has no `close_agent`. Finished children are
  evicted automatically when slots are needed; leaving them unclosed
  costs nothing. Only V1 sessions have `close_agent` — there, close
  reviewers when their review returns, and close each implementer
  after its task's review passes.
- **Model names:** never copy a model name from a skill, table, or old
  session into `spawn_agent` without checking it against your current
  spawn allowlist — V2 accepts only V2-capable presets and hard-errors
  on the rest.

## Waiting on children

`wait_agent` is an event subscription, not a poll: a long wait wakes
the moment a child produces mailbox activity, with the same latency as
a short one. Short-timeout polling buys nothing and costs a tool call —
and a context rebill — per poll. In measured sessions, roughly
two-thirds of all wait calls were short polls that timed out.

- While you still have local work, do not wait at all. A completed
  child's final answer is pushed into your mailbox and arrives with
  your next turn.
- When you are genuinely idle with children outstanding, wait in
  bounded stretches: `wait_agent` with `timeout_ms` 300000-600000
  (5-10 minutes). After each stretch — wake or timeout — post one
  status line, run `list_agents`, and chase any child that finished
  without reporting. Never stack polls shorter than five minutes; the
  event subscription wakes a bounded stretch just as fast as a short
  one.
- Completion mail cannot wake an idle controller (it is delivered
  without triggering a turn); covering that idle window is
  `wait_agent`'s only job. A stretch that times out with no activity
  is your cue to reconcile, not to shorten the next stretch.

## Model routing on spawns

Every `spawn_agent` you issue — including when you are yourself a
spawned child running a fan-out — sets `model` AND `reasoning_effort`
explicitly, per the Model Selection rules of the skill you are
executing. Setting `model` alone is a trap: the child's effort
silently resets to that model's default, not to yours.

Ask your human partner to add a machine-level backstop to
`~/.codex/config.toml` so any spawn that slips through still routes to
a deliberate tier instead of silently inheriting the session's most
expensive model:

```toml
[agents]
default_subagent_model = "<a mid-tier model from your spawn allowlist>"
default_subagent_reasoning_effort = "medium"
```

## Environment Detection

Skills that create worktrees or finish branches should detect their
environment with read-only git commands before proceeding:

```bash
GIT_DIR=$(cd "$(git rev-parse --git-dir)" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P)
GIT_COMMON=$(cd "$(git rev-parse --git-common-dir)" 2>/dev/null && pwd -P)
BRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)
```

- `GIT_DIR != GIT_COMMON` → already in a linked worktree (skip creation)
- `BRANCH` empty → detached HEAD (cannot branch/push/PR from sandbox)

See `using-git-worktrees` Step 0 and `finishing-a-development-branch`
Step 1 for how each skill uses these signals.

## Codex App Finishing

When the sandbox blocks branch/push operations (detached HEAD in an
externally managed worktree), the agent commits all work and informs
the user to use the App's native controls:

- **"Create branch"** — names the branch, then commit/push/PR via App UI
- **"Hand off to local"** — transfers work to the user's local checkout

The agent can still run tests, stage files, and output suggested branch
names, commit messages, and PR descriptions for the user to copy.

   555	        .task(id: (object.map { ObjectWords($0).busy } ?? false) && scenePhase == .active) {
   556	            guard let object, ObjectWords(object).busy, scenePhase == .active else { return }
   557	            while !Task.isCancelled {
   558	                try? await Task.sleep(for: .seconds(3))
   559	                await reload()
   560	            }
   561	        }
   562	    }
   563	
   564	    private func reload() async {
   565	        do { store.replace(try await store.mac.get(id)) }
   566	        catch let failure as MacError { problem = failure.message }
   567	        catch { problem = MacError.unreachable.message }
   568	    }
   569	
   570	    private func act(_ body: @escaping () async throws -> StmObject?) {
   571	        guard !working else { return }
   572	        working = true; problem = nil
   573	        Task {
   574	            defer { working = false }
   575	            do { if let next = try await body() { store.replace(next) } else { await reload() } }
   576	            catch let failure as MacError {
   577	                if failure.code == "cancelled" { return }
   578	                if failure.needsPairing { store.onPair(); return }
   579	                problem = failure.message
   580	            } catch { problem = MacError.unreachable.message }
   581	        }
   582	    }
   583	
   584	    @ViewBuilder private func page(_ object: StmObject) -> some View {
   585	        let words = ObjectWords(object)
   586	        List {
   587	            if object.current != nil || words.busy {
   588	                Section {
   589	                    Renders(store: store, object: object)
   590	                        .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets())
   591	                }
   592	            }
   593	            Section {
   594	                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
   595	                    if let size = words.size {
   596	                        Text(size).font(.system(size: 34, weight: .bold, design: .rounded)).monospacedDigit()
   597	                    }
   598	                    HStack(spacing: 8) {
   599	                        if words.busy { ProgressView() }
   600	                        Text(words.status).font(.body.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(words.tone.color)
   601	                    }
   602	                    if let summary = object.current?.summary, !words.busy {
   603	                        Text(summary).font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(.secondary)
   604	                    }
   605	                }
   606	                .padding(.vertical, 4)
   607	                if object.plate?.photo == true, let plateImage {
   608	                    Image(uiImage: plateImage).resizable().scaledToFit()
   609	                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
   610	                        .accessibilityLabel("The printer camera's view of the plate")
   611	                }
   612	                if let problem { Label(problem, systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill").foregroundStyle(.red) }
   613	            }
   614	            if !words.actions.filter({ $0 != .correct }).isEmpty {
   615	                Section { actions(object, words) }
   616	                    .listRowBackground(Color.clear)
   617	                    .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets())
   618	            }
   619	            if words.actions.contains(.correct) {
   620	                Section {
   621	                    TextField("Say what's wrong, like a size or a missing part", text: $note, axis: .vertical).lineLimit(2...6)
   622	                    Button("Send Changes") {
   623	                        let text = note.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
   624	                        act { let next = try await store.mac.correct(object.id, text); note = ""; return next }
   625	                    }
   626	                    .disabled(working || note.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty)
   627	                } header: { Text("Anything to Fix?") } footer: { Text("It goes back to your Mac for another touch-up.") }
   628	            }
   629	            Section("What You Asked For") {
   630	                Text((object.instructions ?? "").isEmpty ? "A copy of what you scanned." : object.instructions!)
   631	                LabeledContent("Sent", value: ObjectWords.sent(object))
   632	            }
   633	        }
   634	        .navigationTitle(words.title)
   635	        .toolbar {
   636	            ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
   637	                Menu {
   638	                    if object.current != nil {
   639	                        Button("Turn It Around", systemImage: "rotate.3d") { act { try await store.mac.showModel(object); return nil } }
   640	                    }
   641	                    Button("Delete", systemImage: "trash", role: .destructive) { confirmDelete = true }
   642	                } label: { Label("More", systemImage: "ellipsis") }
   643	            }
   644	        }
   645	        .confirmationDialog("Delete this from your Mac?", isPresented: $confirmDelete, titleVisibility: .visible) {
   646	            Button("Delete", role: .destructive) {
   647	                Task {
   648	                    do { try await store.mac.delete(object.id); store.remove(object.id); close() }
   649	                    catch let failure as MacError { problem = failure.message }
   650	                }
   651	            }
   652	        } message: { Text("Its model, photos and renders are removed from your Mac.") }
   653	        .task(id: "\(object.updatedAtMs)#\(object.plate?.photo == true)") {
   654	            guard object.plate?.photo == true, let url = try? await store.mac.download(object, "plate.jpg"),
   655	                  let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url) else { plateImage = nil; return }
   656	            plateImage = UIImage(data: data)
   657	        }
   658	    }
   659	
   660	    @ViewBuilder private func actions(_ object: StmObject, _ words: ObjectWords) -> some View {
   661	        VStack(spacing: 10) {
   662	            ForEach(Array(words.actions.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, action in
   663	                switch action {
   664	                case .print:
   665	                    big("Print", "printer.fill", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.print(object.id, checked: false) }
   666	                case .printChecked:
   667	                    big("It Isn't Printing, Print Again", "printer.fill", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.print(object.id, checked: true) }
   668	                case .plateClear:
   669	                    big("It's Actually Clear", "checkmark.circle", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.plateClear(object.id) }
   670	                case .openCamera:
   671	                    big("Open Camera", "camera", prominent: true) {
   672	                        let sent = try await store.mac.addCloseups(object.id, camera: true)
   673	                        guard sent > 0 else { return nil }
   674	                        return try await store.mac.closeup(object.id, bestGuess: false)
   675	                    }
   676	                case .attachPhotos:
   677	                    big(object.state == .needsCloseup ? "Attach Photos" : "Add Photos", "photo.on.rectangle", prominent: false) {
   678	                        let sent = try await store.mac.addCloseups(object.id, camera: false)
   679	                        guard sent > 0 else { return nil }
   680	                        if object.state == .needsCloseup { return try await store.mac.closeup(object.id, bestGuess: false) }
   681	                        return try await store.mac.start(object.id)
   682	                    }
   683	                case .bestGuess:
   684	                    big("Make Your Best Guess", "sparkles", prominent: false) { try await store.mac.closeup(object.id, bestGuess: true) }
   685	                case .retry:
   686	                    big("Try Again", "arrow.clockwise", prominent: true) { try await store.mac.start(object.id) }
   687	                case .finishSending:
   688	                    big("Finish Sending", "arrow.up.circle", prominent: false) {
   689	                        if let run = await store.mac.finishedScan(object.id) { return try await store.mac.sendScan(object.id, run: run) }
   690	                        return try await store.mac.start(object.id)
   691	                    }
   692	                case .scan:
   693	                    if store.canScan {
   694	                        big("Scan It", "viewfinder", prominent: true) {
   695	                            guard let run = try await store.mac.scan(object.id) else { return nil }
   696	                            return try await store.mac.sendScan(object.id, run: run)
   697	                        }
   698	                    }
   699	                case .makeIt:
   700	                    big("Make It From the Description", "wand.and.sparkles", prominent: false) { try await store.mac.start(object.id) }
   701	                case .correct:
   702	                    EmptyView()
   703	                }
   704	            }
   705	        }
   706	        .padding(.vertical, 4)
   707	    }
   708	
   709	    private func big(_ title: String, _ symbol: String, prominent: Bool, _ body: @escaping () async throws -> StmObject?) -> some View {
   710	        Group {
   711	            if prominent {
   712	                Button { act(body) } label: { Label(title, systemImage: symbol).frame(maxWidth: .infinity) }.buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
   713	            } else {
   714	                Button { act(body) } label: { Label(title, systemImage: symbol).frame(maxWidth: .infinity) }.buttonStyle(.glass)
   715	            }
   716	        }
   717	        .controlSize(.large)
   718	        .disabled(working)
   719	        .accessibilityLabel(title)
   720	    }

---BASE STATUS / JSON FIELDS---
  }
  return object.scan ? 'Copy of your scan' : 'Something new';
}

export function formatSize(size: [number, number, number]): string {
  return `${size.map(n => Math.round(n)).join(' × ')} mm`;
}

export function describeObject(object: StmObject): ObjectView {
  const title = objectTitle(object);
  const size = object.current?.sizeMm
    ? `${formatSize(object.current.sizeMm)}, ready to print`
    : undefined;
  const view = (
    status: string,
    tone: ObjectView['tone'],
    actions: ObjectAction[] = [],
    busy = false,
  ): ObjectView => ({ title, status, tone, actions, busy, size });
  switch (object.state) {
    case 'new':
      // A send that stopped part way can always be finished from here.
      if (object.scan || object.photos.length)
        return view('Sending to your Mac…', 'pending', ['finishSending'], true);
      return view(
        'Waiting for a scan or photos.',
        'neutral',
        object.instructions?.trim()
          ? ['scan', 'attachPhotos', 'makeIt']
          : ['scan', 'attachPhotos'],
      );
    case 'touching-up': {
      const changing = object.passes.length > 1 && !!object.current;
      const what = changing
        ? 'Making your changes.'
        : object.scan
        ? 'Touching up your scan.'
        : 'Making it.';
      return view(`${what} ${WAIT}`, 'pending', [], true);
    }
    case 'needs-closeup':
      return view(
        `Can you send me a close-up of ${
          object.question?.closeup ?? 'the part I missed'
        }? I can't see what's happening there.`,
        'accent',
        ['openCamera', 'attachPhotos', 'bestGuess'],
      );
    case 'ready':
      return view('Ready to print', 'pass', ['print', 'correct']);
    case 'printing':
      if (object.plate?.noCamera)
        return view(
          'Check the plate is clear.',
          'accent',
          ['plateClear'],
          true,
        );
      if (object.plate)
        return view(
          `Looks like there's ${
            object.plate.item ?? 'something'
          } on your plate. Can you take it off first?`,
          'accent',
          ['plateClear'],
          true,
        );
      return view('Printing', 'pending', [], true);
    case 'printed':
      return view('Printed', 'pass', ['print', 'correct']);
    case 'failed':
      return view(
        object.failure?.trim() || 'Something went wrong on your Mac.',
        'fail',
        object.print?.uncertain
          ? ['printChecked', 'correct']
          : object.current
          ? ['print', 'correct']
          : ['retry'],
      );
  }
}

import React from 'react';
import ReactTestRenderer, { act } from 'react-test-renderer';
import { Linking, Modal, Text } from 'react-native';
import App from '../App';

function texts(tree: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer): string[] {
  return tree.root
    .findAllByType(Text)
    .map(t => [t.props.children].flat().join(''));
}
async function render() {
  let tree!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
  await act(async () => {
    tree = ReactTestRenderer.create(<App />);
  });
  return tree;
}
afterEach(() => jest.restoreAllMocks());

test('the shell shows the native app, and says what it needs where there is none', async () => {
  jest.spyOn(Linking, 'getInitialURL').mockResolvedValue(null);
  const tree = await render();
  // Jest has no native SwiftUI view, so the shell's fallback shows.
  expect(texts(tree)).toContain('Scan to Make needs an iPhone or iPad.');
  expect(tree.root.findAllByType(Modal)).toHaveLength(0);
  await act(async () => tree.unmount());
});

        '&e=9999999999999&f=' +
        'b'.repeat(64),
    );
  const tree = await render();
  expect(tree.root.findAllByType(Modal)).toHaveLength(1);
  await act(async () => tree.unmount());
});

name: swiftui-liquid-glass
description: Implement and review iOS 26+ SwiftUI Liquid Glass UI. Use when adopting Liquid Glass or checking its correctness, performance, and design fit.
---

# SwiftUI Liquid Glass

## Overview
Use this skill to build or review SwiftUI features that fully align with the iOS 26+ Liquid Glass API. Prioritize native APIs (`glassEffect`, `GlassEffectContainer`, glass button styles) and Apple design guidance. Keep usage consistent, interactive where needed, and performance aware.

## Workflow Decision Tree
Choose the path that matches the request:

### 1) Review an existing feature
- Inspect where Liquid Glass should be used and where it should not.
- Verify correct modifier order, shape usage, and container placement.
- Check for iOS 26+ availability handling and sensible fallbacks.

### 2) Improve a feature using Liquid Glass
- Identify target components for glass treatment (surfaces, chips, buttons, cards).
- Refactor to use `GlassEffectContainer` where multiple glass elements appear.
- Introduce interactive glass only for tappable or focusable elements.

### 3) Implement a new feature using Liquid Glass
- Design the glass surfaces and interactions first (shape, prominence, grouping).
- Add glass modifiers after layout/appearance modifiers.
- Add morphing transitions only when the view hierarchy changes with animation.

## Core Guidelines
- Prefer native Liquid Glass APIs over custom blurs.
- Use `GlassEffectContainer` when multiple glass elements coexist.
- Apply `.glassEffect(...)` after layout and visual modifiers.
- Use `.interactive()` for elements that respond to touch/pointer.
- Keep shapes consistent across related elements for a cohesive look.
- Gate with `#available(iOS 26, *)` and provide a non-glass fallback.

## Review Checklist
- **Availability**: `#available(iOS 26, *)` present with fallback UI.
- **Composition**: Multiple glass views wrapped in `GlassEffectContainer`.
- **Modifier order**: `glassEffect` applied after layout/appearance modifiers.
- **Interactivity**: `interactive()` only where user interaction exists.
- **Transitions**: `glassEffectID` used with `@Namespace` for morphing.
- **Consistency**: Shapes, tinting, and spacing align across the feature.

## Implementation Checklist
- Define target elements and desired glass prominence.
- Wrap grouped glass elements in `GlassEffectContainer` and tune spacing.
- Use `.glassEffect(.regular.tint(...).interactive(), in: .rect(cornerRadius: ...))` as needed.
- Use `.buttonStyle(.glass)` / `.buttonStyle(.glassProminent)` for actions.
- Add morphing transitions with `glassEffectID` when hierarchy changes.
- Provide fallback materials and visuals for earlier iOS versions.

## Quick Snippets
Use these patterns directly and tailor shapes/tints/spacing.

```swift
if #available(iOS 26, *) {
    Text("Hello")
        .padding()
        .glassEffect(.regular.interactive(), in: .rect(cornerRadius: 16))
} else {
    Text("Hello")
        .padding()
        .background(.ultraThinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16))
}
```

```swift
GlassEffectContainer(spacing: 24) {
    HStack(spacing: 24) {
        Image(systemName: "scribble.variable")
            .frame(width: 72, height: 72)
            .font(.system(size: 32))
            .glassEffect()
        Image(systemName: "eraser.fill")
            .frame(width: 72, height: 72)
            .font(.system(size: 32))
            .glassEffect()
    }
}
```

```swift
Button("Confirm") { }
    .buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
```

## Resources
- Reference guide: `references/liquid-glass.md`
- Prefer Apple docs for up-to-date API details, and use web search to consult current Apple Developer documentation in addition to the references above.

+import AppKit
+
+// Scan to Make mark: a viewfinder (scan) framing an isometric cube (make), white on cobalt.
+func draw(size: Int, rounded: Bool, to path: String) {
+    let s = CGFloat(size)
+    let rep = NSBitmapImageRep(bitmapDataPlanes:nil, pixelsWide:size, pixelsHigh:size, bitsPerSample:8, samplesPerPixel:4,
+                               hasAlpha:true, isPlanar:false, colorSpaceName:.deviceRGB, bytesPerRow:0, bitsPerPixel:0)!
+    NSGraphicsContext.saveGraphicsState()
+    NSGraphicsContext.current = NSGraphicsContext(bitmapImageRep:rep)
+    let ctx = NSGraphicsContext.current!.cgContext
+    let cobalt = CGColor(red:0x24/255, green:0x48/255, blue:0xD8/255, alpha:1)
+    let deep = CGColor(red:0x1B/255, green:0x37/255, blue:0xA8/255, alpha:1)
+    let rect = CGRect(x:0, y:0, width:s, height:s)
+    if rounded { ctx.addPath(CGPath(roundedRect:rect, cornerWidth:s * 0.225, cornerHeight:s * 0.225, transform:nil)); ctx.clip() }
+    // A quiet vertical gradient so it isn't a flat swatch.
+    let gradient = CGGradient(colorsSpace:CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceRGB(), colors:[cobalt, deep] as CFArray, locations:[0, 1])!
+    ctx.drawLinearGradient(gradient, start:CGPoint(x:0, y:s), end:CGPoint(x:0, y:0), options:[])
+    ctx.setStrokeColor(CGColor(gray:1, alpha:1))
+    ctx.setLineCap(.round); ctx.setLineJoin(.round)
+    // Viewfinder corners.
+    let inset = s * 0.18, arm = s * 0.15, w = s * 0.055
+    ctx.setLineWidth(w)
+    for (cx, cy, dx, dy) in [(inset, inset, 1.0, 1.0), (s - inset, inset, -1.0, 1.0), (inset, s - inset, 1.0, -1.0), (s - inset, s - inset, -1.0, -1.0)] {
+        ctx.move(to:CGPoint(x:cx, y:cy + dy * arm)); ctx.addLine(to:CGPoint(x:cx, y:cy)); ctx.addLine(to:CGPoint(x:cx + dx * arm, y:cy))
+        ctx.strokePath()
+    }
+    // Isometric cube, centred.
+    let c = CGPoint(x:s / 2, y:s / 2 - s * 0.01), r = s * 0.2
+    let cos30 = CGFloat(0.8660254)
+    let top = CGPoint(x:c.x, y:c.y + r), bottom = CGPoint(x:c.x, y:c.y - r)
+    let ul = CGPoint(x:c.x - r * cos30, y:c.y + r / 2), ur = CGPoint(x:c.x + r * cos30, y:c.y + r / 2)
+    let ll = CGPoint(x:c.x - r * cos30, y:c.y - r / 2), lr = CGPoint(x:c.x + r * cos30, y:c.y - r / 2)
+    // Top face filled lightly, so it reads as a solid printed part.
+    ctx.setFillColor(CGColor(gray:1, alpha:0.28))
+    ctx.move(to:top); ctx.addLine(to:ur); ctx.addLine(to:c); ctx.addLine(to:ul); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillPath()
+    ctx.setLineWidth(s * 0.045)
+    ctx.move(to:top); ctx.addLine(to:ur); ctx.addLine(to:lr); ctx.addLine(to:bottom); ctx.addLine(to:ll); ctx.addLine(to:ul); ctx.closePath()
+    ctx.move(to:ul); ctx.addLine(to:c); ctx.addLine(to:ur)
+    ctx.move(to:c); ctx.addLine(to:bottom)
+    ctx.strokePath()
+    NSGraphicsContext.restoreGraphicsState()
+    try! rep.representation(using:.png, properties:[:])!.write(to:URL(fileURLWithPath:path))
+}
+let out = CommandLine.arguments[1]
+draw(size:1024, rounded:false, to:out + "/AppIcon-1024.png")
+for (scale, px) in [(1, 44), (2, 88), (3, 132)] { draw(size:px, rounded:true, to:out + "/logo" + (scale == 1 ? "" : "@\(scale)x") + ".png") }
+draw(size:512, rounded:true, to:out + "/preview.png")

@interface ObjectsBridge : NSObject <RCTBridgeModule, PHPickerViewControllerDelegate, UIImagePickerControllerDelegate, UINavigationControllerDelegate, UIAdaptivePresentationControllerDelegate>
@property NSMutableSet<NSString *> *uploading;
@property NSString *pickObject;

@property(copy) RCTPromiseRejectBlock pickReject;
@end

@implementation ObjectsBridge
RCT_EXPORT_MODULE()
+ (BOOL)requiresMainQueueSetup { return NO; }
- (dispatch_queue_t)methodQueue { return dispatch_get_main_queue(); }
- (instancetype)init { if ((self = [super init])) _uploading = [NSMutableSet set]; return self; }

static NSString *STMObjectsMessage(NSString *code) {
  NSDictionary *messages = @{
    @"pairing-required": @"Connect to your Mac again.", @"scope-denied": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
    @"pairing-changed": @"Connect to your Mac again.",
    @"certificate-changed": @"Your Mac presented a different certificate. Scan a new QR code from it.",
    @"unreachable": @"Your Mac isn't answering. Check it's on and awake, then try again.",
    @"payload-too-large": @"That file is too large to send.",
    @"invalid-request": @"That request isn't allowed.",
  };
  return messages[code] ?: @"Something went wrong talking to your Mac. Try again.";
}

RCT_REMAP_METHOD(call, callOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
  NSString *method = o[@"method"], *path = o[@"path"], *scope = STMObjectsPath(path, method), *code = nil;
  id json = o[@"json"];
  if (!scope || ([method isEqual:@"GET"] && json)) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  NSDictionary *pairing = STMObjectsPairing(scope, &code);
  if (!pairing) { reject(code, STMObjectsMessage(code), nil); return; }
  NSData *body = nil;
  if (json) {
    if (![NSJSONSerialization isValidJSONObject:json]) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
    body = [NSJSONSerialization dataWithJSONObject:json options:0 error:nil];
    if (!body || body.length > 16 * 1024) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
  }
  [STMObjectsRequest send:method path:path body:body type:@"application/json" pairing:pairing limit:STMObjectsJSONLimit done:^(NSInteger status, NSData *data, NSString *failure) {

import UIKit
import React
import React_RCTAppDelegate
import ReactAppDependencyProvider

@main
class AppDelegate: UIResponder, UIApplicationDelegate {
  var reactNativeDelegate: ReactNativeDelegate?
  var reactNativeFactory: RCTReactNativeFactory?
  var launchOptions: [UIApplication.LaunchOptionsKey: Any]?

  func application(
    _ application: UIApplication,
    didFinishLaunchingWithOptions launchOptions: [UIApplication.LaunchOptionsKey: Any]? = nil
  ) -> Bool {
    let delegate = ReactNativeDelegate()
    let factory = RCTReactNativeFactory(delegate: delegate)
    delegate.dependencyProvider = RCTAppDependencyProvider()

    reactNativeDelegate = delegate
    reactNativeFactory = factory
    self.launchOptions = launchOptions

    return true
  }
}

// The scene owns the window. Resizing or moving between displays changes its
// geometry without creating a new React root or discarding an in-progress draft.
class SceneDelegate: UIResponder, UIWindowSceneDelegate {
  var window: UIWindow?

  func scene(
    _ scene: UIScene,
    willConnectTo session: UISceneSession,
    options connectionOptions: UIScene.ConnectionOptions
  ) {
    guard let windowScene = scene as? UIWindowScene,
          let appDelegate = UIApplication.shared.delegate as? AppDelegate,
          let factory = appDelegate.reactNativeFactory else { return }

    let window = UIWindow(windowScene: windowScene)
    self.window = window

    // A pairing link that launched the app reaches Linking.getInitialURL.
    var options = appDelegate.launchOptions ?? [:]
    if let url = connectionOptions.urlContexts.first?.url {
      options[.url] = url
    }
    factory.startReactNative(
      withModuleName: "ScanToMake",
      in: window,
      launchOptions: options
    )
  }

  // Links opened while the app runs, e.g. a pairing QR read by the Camera app.
  func scene(_ scene: UIScene, openURLContexts URLContexts: Set<UIOpenURLContext>) {
    guard let url = URLContexts.first?.url else { return }
    RCTLinkingManager.application(UIApplication.shared, open: url, options: [:])
  }
}

class ReactNativeDelegate: RCTDefaultReactNativeFactoryDelegate {
  override func sourceURL(for bridge: RCTBridge) -> URL? {
    self.bundleURL()
  }

  override func bundleURL() -> URL? {
#if DEBUG
    RCTBundleURLProvider.sharedSettings().jsBundleURL(forBundleRoot: "index")
#else
    Bundle.main.url(forResource: "main", withExtension: "jsbundle")
#endif
  }
}

# M2b: Phone App Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax.

**Goal:** Replace every screen of the iPhone app with the spec §5 experience on top of the M2a `/v1/objects` API:
- the objects list;
- start something (instructions, then Scan or Photos);
- the object page (state, renders, size, Print, corrections, close-up ask, plate check).

The QR pairing (`FindYourMacScreen` and its native modules) and the native Object Capture module stay as they are.

**Architecture:**
- **New native bridge** `ObjectsBridge.mm` in `modules/local-project-storage/ios/`. It does pinned HTTPS to `/v1/objects/**`, copying `StillsProcessingBridge`'s request/pinning pattern: `STMReadPairing`, `STMPairingBaseURL`, `STMPinnedTrust`, `STMSamePairing`, `STMTransportError`.
  - It also sends a finished Object Capture run's USDZ and photos straight from `Documents/NativeCaptures/<runID>/` (`Models/model-mobile.usdz` and `Images/*.HEIC`), so no file paths reach JS.
  - It downloads renders to the cache and returns file URIs.
- **JS:** `src/objects/` (client and model) plus three new screens. `App.tsx` routes only `objects | start | object`, with pairing as the overlay it already is.

**Tech Stack:** React Native (existing shell), Objective-C bridge, jest, T3 device tools (`agent-device`) on the iOS 27 simulator, David's iPhone for the real run.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-31-app-rebuild-design.md` §5.1–5.5, §8.

## Global Constraints

- **Paths:** only `/v1/objects`, `/v1/objects/<uuid>` and `/v1/objects/<uuid>/(scan|photos?name=…|start|corrections|closeup|print|plate|model.stl|renders/<name>.png)`. Everything else is refused in native code.
- **Scopes:** reads `projects:read`, writes `captures:import`, print `printer-handoff:use`.
- **Photos from a scan:** up to 24, evenly spread from first to last. HEIC as captured; the Mac converts.
- **Copy:** plain words, the states listed in spec §5.1, and no "companion", "MCP" or "job" anywhere in UI copy. Size is shown as "W × D × H mm".
- **Layouts:** Duo folded and iPhone are one column. Open Duo and iPad are list plus page side by side. Devices without LiDAR hide Scan and say scanning needs a LiDAR device.
- **Polling:** while an object is `touching-up` or `printing`, poll `GET /v1/objects/<id>` every 3 s, and only while the screen is visible. Notifications are a later step.
- Delete the old screens and their clients and tests once the new ones work: RetainedCaptures, ScanResults, Print, CloseUps, ProjectsHome, Settings flows, Compare versions.

## Review Focus

1. **Pairing lost or certificate changed:** the bridge rejects with `pairing-required` or `certificate-changed`, and the app shows Find your Mac, not a raw error.
2. **A scan run with no photos, or a missing USDZ:** the upload reports which one is missing. It never starts a touch-up with no inputs.
3. **The app is backgrounded mid-upload:** the upload is resumable by object id. On return, the phone re-sends whatever the object lacks, comparing photo names, then calls start.
4. **Two taps on Print or Send:** one request only.
5. **The Mac is asleep or unreachable:** each screen shows one sentence and Try again.

---

### Task 1: Native `ObjectsBridge`
**Files:** create `modules/local-project-storage/ios/ObjectsBridge.mm` and register it like the other bridges; JS wrapper `src/objects/native.ts`.
**Produces** (JS):
- `ObjectsBridge.call({method: 'GET'|'POST'|'PUT', path, json?})` → `{status, body}`;
- `ObjectsBridge.sendCapture({objectId, runID, maxPhotos})` → `{photosSent, scanSent}`;
- `ObjectsBridge.download({objectId, name})` → `{uri}`.

**Tests:** extend `scripts/native-stills-processing-tests` (its Python harness with `scripts/pairing-fixture/server.mjs`) with objects-route cases:
- allowlist accept and reject;
- a pin mismatch gives `certificate-changed`;
- `sendCapture` with a fixture capture folder sends `scan` and N photos, then `start`;
- `download` returns a cached file whose bytes match.

### Task 2: JS client and view model
**Files:** `src/objects/client.ts`, `src/objects/model.ts`. Tests: `__tests__/objectsModel.test.ts`.
**Produces:**
- `objectsClient` with `list`, `get`, `create(instructions)`, `sendCapture`, `start`, `correct`, `closeupBestGuess`, `print`, `plateClear` and `renderUri`;
- `describe(object)` → `{title, status line, primary action}`. It maps every state, plate case and failure to spec §5 copy.

**Tests:** every state gets its line; `needs-closeup` shows the question; `plate.item` shows "Looks like there's a … on your plate"; `plate.noCamera` shows "Check the plate is clear"; a failure shows its reason with Try again; the size format.

### Task 3: Screens
**Files:** `src/screens/ObjectsScreen.tsx`, `src/screens/StartScreen.tsx`, `src/screens/ObjectScreen.tsx`; `App.tsx` routes. Tests: `__tests__/screens.test.tsx` (render with a fake client).
- **Objects:** a list of cards (render image, title, status line), one Scan or New button, and the Mac pill (existing pairing status).
- **Start:** an instructions box ("What do you want to make?", optional), then **Scan** (opens the existing native capture) or **Use photos** (system picker), or **Make it** (instructions only). After capture: create the object, `sendCapture`, start, and go to the object page.
- **Object:**
  - the state line;
  - the renders (swipeable) and the size;
  - **Print**;
  - the corrections box;
  - the close-up ask with Open camera / Attach photos / Make your best guess;
  - the plate photo with "It's actually clear".

### Task 4: Delete the old UI, and run it
- Remove the old screens, clients and their tests; `npm test`, `tsc` and lint stay green.
- Build and install on the iOS 27 simulator with T3's device tools, and drive Start → Make it (instructions only) → object page end to end against the live companion on the Mac. Screenshots.
- Then install on David's phone and do a real scan.

import UIKit
import React

/// The native control owns its material, interaction and accessibility behavior.
@objc(STMActionButton)
public final class STMActionButton: UIView {
  private let control = UIButton(type: .system)
  private let measureLabel = UILabel()
  private var reportedHeight: CGFloat = 0

  @objc public var buttonTitle: String = "" { didSet { configure() } }
  @objc public var spokenLabel: String = "" { didSet { configure() } }
  @objc public var hint: String? { didSet { configure() } }
  @objc public var primary: Bool = false { didSet { configure() } }
  @objc public var compact: Bool = false { didSet { configure() } }
  @objc public var disabled: Bool = false { didSet { configure() } }
  /// An SF Symbol shown before the title, or alone when the title is empty.
  @objc public var systemImage: String? { didSet { configure() } }
  @objc public var onPress: RCTDirectEventBlock?
  @objc public var onSizeChange: RCTDirectEventBlock? { didSet { reportedHeight = 0; setNeedsLayout() } }

  public override init(frame: CGRect) {
    super.init(frame: frame)
    addSubview(control)
    control.addTarget(self, action: #selector(activate), for: .primaryActionTriggered)
    // Only the UIButton is an accessibility element. React Native also applies
    // accessibility props to this wrapper, which made VoiceOver and XCUITest
    // find the same button more than once (KAN-187).
    isAccessibilityElement = false
    accessibilityElements = [control]
    configure()
  }

  required init?(coder: NSCoder) { fatalError("init(coder:) has not been implemented") }

  /// The clay fill for primary actions (theme.ts `clay`). White text on it is 4.6:1.
  private static let accent = UIColor { traits in
    traits.userInterfaceStyle == .dark
      ? UIColor(red: 0x7B / 255, green: 0x95 / 255, blue: 0xFF / 255, alpha: 1)
      : UIColor(red: 0x24 / 255, green: 0x48 / 255, blue: 0xD8 / 255, alpha: 1)
  }

  private var iconOnly: Bool { buttonTitle.isEmpty && systemImage != nil }

  private func configure() {
    var config: UIButton.Configuration = primary ? .prominentGlass() : .glass()
    let font = UIFont.preferredFont(forTextStyle: .headline, compatibleWith: traitCollection)
    config.title = buttonTitle.isEmpty ? nil : buttonTitle
    if let systemImage, let image = UIImage(systemName: systemImage) {
      config.image = image
      config.imagePadding = 8
      config.preferredSymbolConfigurationForImage = UIImage.SymbolConfiguration(textStyle: .headline)
    } else {
      config.image = nil
    }
    if iconOnly { config.cornerStyle = .capsule }
    config.titleAlignment = .center
    config.titleLineBreakMode = .byWordWrapping
    config.titleTextAttributesTransformer = UIConfigurationTextAttributesTransformer { attributes in
      var result = attributes
      result.font = font
      return result
    }
    config.contentInsets = iconOnly
      ? NSDirectionalEdgeInsets(top: 8, leading: 8, bottom: 8, trailing: 8)
      : NSDirectionalEdgeInsets(top: compact ? 8 : 14, leading: compact ? 12 : 20,
                                bottom: compact ? 8 : 14, trailing: compact ? 12 : 20)
    control.configuration = config
    // Primary actions use the app's cobalt (theme.ts `accent`): the one colour for moving something forward.
    control.tintColor = primary ? Self.accent : nil
    control.isEnabled = !disabled
    control.titleLabel?.numberOfLines = 0
    control.accessibilityLabel = spokenLabel.isEmpty ? buttonTitle : spokenLabel
    control.accessibilityHint = hint
    accessibilityLabel = nil
    accessibilityHint = nil
    accessibilityTraits = []
    measureLabel.font = font
    measureLabel.text = buttonTitle
    measureLabel.numberOfLines = 0
    measureLabel.lineBreakMode = .byWordWrapping
    setNeedsLayout()
  }

   300	}
   301	
   302	#pragma mark Photos chosen on the start screen
   303	
   304	// Photos picked before anything is sent: kept on this phone until Make it, shown from their cache
   305	// file, then sent to the new object. Their ids are `<uuid>.<ext>` in Caches/ObjectsChosen.
   306	static NSURL *STMObjectsChosenRoot(void) { return STMObjectsRoot(NSCachesDirectory, @"ObjectsChosen"); }
   307	
   308	RCT_REMAP_METHOD(choosePhotos, choosePhotosOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
   309	  NSInteger limit = [o[@"limit"] integerValue];
   310	  UIViewController *presenter = RCTPresentedViewController();
   311	  if (limit < 1 || limit > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
   312	  if ([self sheetBusy:presenter]) { reject(@"busy", @"Close the current sheet first.", nil); return; }
   313	  PHPickerConfiguration *configuration = [[PHPickerConfiguration alloc] init];
   314	  configuration.filter = [PHPickerFilter imagesFilter]; configuration.selectionLimit = limit;
   315	  configuration.preferredAssetRepresentationMode = PHPickerConfigurationAssetRepresentationModeCurrent;
   316	  PHPickerViewController *picker = [[PHPickerViewController alloc] initWithConfiguration:configuration];
   317	  picker.delegate = self; picker.presentationController.delegate = self;
   318	  self.pickObject = nil; self.pickResolve = resolve; self.pickReject = reject;
   319	  [presenter presentViewController:picker animated:YES completion:nil];
   320	}
   321	
   322	/** Keeps chosen photos in the cache and resolves their ids and display URIs. */
   323	- (void)finishChosen:(NSArray<NSDictionary *> *)photos {
   324	  RCTPromiseResolveBlock resolve = self.pickResolve; self.pickResolve = nil; self.pickReject = nil;
   325	  if (!resolve) return;
   326	  NSURL *root = STMObjectsChosenRoot();
   327	  [NSFileManager.defaultManager createDirectoryAtURL:root withIntermediateDirectories:YES attributes:nil error:nil];
   328	  NSMutableArray *chosen = [NSMutableArray array];
   329	  for (NSDictionary *photo in photos) {
   330	    NSString *name = [NSString stringWithFormat:@"%@.%@", NSUUID.UUID.UUIDString.lowercaseString, [photo[@"name"] pathExtension]];
   331	    NSURL *url = [root URLByAppendingPathComponent:name];
   332	    if ([photo[@"bytes"] writeToURL:url options:NSDataWritingAtomic error:nil]) [chosen addObject:@{@"id": name, @"uri": url.absoluteString}];
   333	  }
   334	  resolve(chosen);
   335	}
   336	
   337	RCT_REMAP_METHOD(sendChosen, sendChosenOptions:(NSDictionary *)o resolve:(RCTPromiseResolveBlock)resolve reject:(RCTPromiseRejectBlock)reject) {
   338	  NSString *objectId = o[@"objectId"]; NSArray *ids = o[@"ids"];
   339	  if (!STMObjectsMatches(objectId, STMObjectsUUID) || ![ids isKindOfClass:NSArray.class] || ids.count > 24) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
   340	  NSMutableArray *files = [NSMutableArray array];
   341	  for (id chosen in ids) {
   342	    if (!STMObjectsMatches(chosen, @"[a-f0-9-]{36}\\.(jpg|png|heic)")) { reject(@"invalid-request", STMObjectsMessage(@"invalid-request"), nil); return; }
   343	    NSString *ext = [chosen pathExtension];
   344	    NSString *type = [ext isEqual:@"png"] ? @"image/png" : [ext isEqual:@"heic"] ? @"image/heic" : @"image/jpeg";
   345	    [files addObject:@{@"name": [@"photo-" stringByAppendingString:chosen], @"url": [STMObjectsChosenRoot() URLByAppendingPathComponent:chosen], @"type": type}];
   346	  }
   347	  if (!files.count) { resolve(@{@"photosSent": @0}); return; }
   348	  [self sendFiles:files objectId:objectId resolve:^{
   349	    for (NSDictionary *file in files) [NSFileManager.defaultManager removeItemAtURL:file[@"url"] error:nil];
   350	    resolve(@{@"photosSent": @(files.count)});
   351	  } reject:reject];
   352	}
   353	
   354	#pragma mark Downloads and the 3D view
   355	
   356	/** Downloads one object file into the cache (once per object version) and returns its URL. */
   357	- (void)fetch:(NSString *)objectId name:(NSString *)name version:(NSString *)version done:(void (^)(NSURL *url, NSString *code))done {
   358	  NSString *code = nil;
   359	  NSDictionary *pairing = STMObjectsPairing([name isEqual:@"plate.jpg"] ? @"printer-handoff:use" : @"projects:read", &code);
   360	  if (!pairing) { done(nil, code); return; }

---NATIVE TEST HARNESS---
"""Bounded real Simulator detail-still and scan-processing bridge tests.

Requires built companion. Uses a separate disposable app/container. Uses real
capture-scoped credentials
against the synthetic pairing fixture (fake converter and scan runner), so
this proves the native transport, not conversion, Blender or physical fit.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import plistlib
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import uuid
import hashlib

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[3]
BUNDLE = 'com.scantomake.stillsprocessingtests'
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
parser.add_argument('--simulator', required=True, help='Already booted Simulator UUID')
parser.add_argument('--evidence', type=Path, required=True)
args = parser.parse_args()
args.evidence.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
env = dict(os.environ, DEVELOPER_DIR='/Applications/Xcode.app/Contents/Developer')

def run(command, **kwargs):
    return subprocess.run(command, env=env, check=True, timeout=120, **kwargs)

simulators = json.loads(subprocess.check_output(['xcrun', 'simctl', 'list', 'devices', '-j'], env=env))
assert any(d['udid'] == args.simulator and d['state'] == 'Booted'
           for group in simulators['devices'].values() for d in group), 'Target must already be booted'

with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='native-stills-processing-') as temp:
    temp = Path(temp)
    fixture_log = open(temp / 'fixture.log', 'w+')
    fixture = subprocess.Popen(['node', str(REPO / 'apps/mobile/scripts/pairing-fixture/server.mjs')],
                               cwd=REPO, env=dict(env, SCAN_STILLS_PROCESSING_FIXTURE='1'),
                               stdout=fixture_log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    fixture_root = None
    installed = False
    try:
        for _ in range(100):
            fixture_log.seek(0)
            lines = fixture_log.read().splitlines()
            if lines:
                try:
                    info = json.loads(lines[0])
                    fixture_root = Path(info['directory'])
                    break
                except (ValueError, KeyError):
                    raise RuntimeError('Fixture failed to start; inspect private temporary log')
            if fixture.poll() is not None:
                raise RuntimeError('Fixture exited before startup')
            time.sleep(.05)
        assert fixture_root, 'Fixture startup timed out'
        invite = json.loads((fixture_root / 'invitation.json').read_text())
        run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        media = json.loads((fixture_root / 'stills-processing.json').read_text())
        config = {'runId':run_id,'invitation':invite,'media':media}
        (args.evidence / 'fixture-contract.json').write_text(json.dumps(media,indent=2))
        app = temp / 'StillsProcessing.app'
        app.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        (app / 'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(config))
        (app / 'config.json').chmod(0o600)
        with open(app / 'Info.plist','wb') as f:
            plistlib.dump({'CFBundleIdentifier':BUNDLE,'CFBundleExecutable':'StillsProcessing','CFBundleName':'Stills Processing Tests','CFBundlePackageType':'APPL','CFBundleVersion':'1','CFBundleShortVersionString':'1.0','MinimumOSVersion':'16.0','UIApplicationSceneManifest':{'UIApplicationSupportsMultipleScenes':False,'UISceneConfigurations':{'UIWindowSceneSessionRoleApplication':[{'UISceneConfigurationName':'Default','UISceneDelegateClassName':'SceneDelegate'}]}},'UIDeviceFamily':[1,2],'LSRequiresIPhoneOS':True,'NSAppTransportSecurity':{'NSAllowsLocalNetworking':True,'NSAllowsArbitraryLoads':True}}, f)
        sdk = subprocess.check_output(['xcrun','--sdk','iphonesimulator','--show-sdk-path'],env=env,text=True).strip()
        entitlements=temp / 'test.entitlements'
        with open(entitlements,'wb') as f:
            plistlib.dump({'application-identifier':'TESTTEAM.'+BUNDLE,'keychain-access-groups':['TESTTEAM.'+BUNDLE],'get-task-allow':True},f)
        with open(args.evidence / 'compile.log','w') as log:
            run(['xcrun','clang++','-fobjc-arc','-fblocks','-target','arm64-apple-ios16.0-simulator','-isysroot',sdk,'-Xlinker','-sectcreate','-Xlinker','__TEXT','-Xlinker','__entitlements','-Xlinker',str(entitlements),'-I',str(HERE),str(HERE / 'runner.mm'),'-o',str(app / 'StillsProcessing'),'-framework','CoreGraphics','-framework','UIKit','-framework','Foundation','-framework','Security','-framework','QuickLook','-framework','UniformTypeIdentifiers','-framework','ImageIO','-framework','PhotosUI','-framework','SceneKit','-framework','ModelIO'],stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        run(['codesign','--force','--sign','-',str(app)],stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        run(['xcrun','simctl','install',args.simulator,str(app)])
        installed = True
        for phase in (1,2):
          with open(args.evidence / ('runtime-'+str(phase)+'.log'),'w') as log:
            completed = subprocess.run(['xcrun','simctl','launch','--console',args.simulator,BUNDLE],env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,timeout=75)
        assert completed.returncode == 0, 'Native host failed; see runtime.log'
        container = Path(subprocess.check_output(['xcrun','simctl','get_app_container',args.simulator,BUNDLE,'data'],env=env,text=True).strip())
        result = json.loads((container / 'Documents/results-2.json').read_text())
        first=json.loads((container / 'Documents/results-1.json').read_text())
        assert result['passed'] and result['runId'] == run_id and result['phase']==2
        assert first['passed'] and first['runId']==run_id and first['phase']==1
        assert first.get('imageUriCacheStates') is True, 'imageUri cache-miss and corrupt-cache states not exercised'
        assert result['jobId'] and first['jobId']==result['jobId']
        assert result['pngSha256']==media['png']['sha256']
        result['bridgeSha256'] = hashlib.sha256((REPO / 'apps/mobile/modules/local-project-storage/ios/StillsProcessingBridge.mm').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        result['simulator'] = args.simulator
        result['source'] = 'synthetic pairing fixture with fake capture converter and scan runner; native transport proof only, no Blender, geometry or physical fit claim'
        (args.evidence / 'results.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + '\n')
        print(json.dumps({'passed':True,'evidence':str(args.evidence.resolve()),'restart':True}))
    finally:
        if installed:
            subprocess.run(['xcrun','simctl','terminate',args.simulator,BUNDLE],env=env,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            subprocess.run(['xcrun','simctl','launch','--console',args.simulator,BUNDLE,'--cleanup'],env=env,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,timeout=20)
            subprocess.run(['xcrun','simctl','uninstall',args.simulator,BUNDLE],env=env,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        fixture.terminate()
        try:
            fixture.wait(timeout=10)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            fixture.kill()
            fixture.wait()
        fixture_log.close()
        if fixture_root:
            shutil.rmtree(fixture_root)

    52	function App() {
    53	  const scheme = useColorScheme();
    54	  const [companionOpen, setCompanionOpen] = useState(false);
    55	  // Bumped when pairing changes, so the app reads the pairing and its objects again.
    56	  const [pairingKey, setPairingKey] = useState(0);
    57	  // With no Mac paired, Find your Mac is the first screen. A pairing that
    58	  // can't be read leaves the app in place.
    59	  const [needsMac, setNeedsMac] = useState(false);
    60	  const [canScan, setCanScan] = useState(false);
    61	  useEffect(() => {
    62	    let live = true;
    63	    companionNative()
    64	      ?.status()
    65	      .then(
    66	        value => live && setNeedsMac(value.state !== 'paired'),
    67	        () => {},
    68	      );
    69	    return () => {
    70	      live = false;
    71	    };
    72	  }, [pairingKey]);
    73	  // True while Find your Mac is the screen on show, so a link goes to it.
    74	  const findingMac = useRef(false);
    75	  findingMac.current = needsMac;
    76	  // scantomake://pair links, e.g. the Mac's QR read by the Camera app. A cold
    77	  // start can deliver the same link twice within moments: drop that repeat.
    78	  const [pairLink, setPairLink] = useState<string | null>(null);
    79	  const lastLink = useRef<{ url: string; at: number } | null>(null);
    80	  useEffect(() => {
    81	    const open = (url?: string | null) => {
    82	      if (!url || !isPairingLink(url)) return;
    83	      const now = Date.now();
    84	      const last = lastLink.current;
    85	      lastLink.current = { url, at: now };
    86	      if (last?.url === url && now - last.at < 2000) return;
    87	      setPairLink(url);
    88	      if (!findingMac.current) setCompanionOpen(true);
    89	    };
    90	    Linking.getInitialURL().then(open, () => {});
    91	    const subscription = Linking.addEventListener('url', event =>
    92	      open(event.url),
    93	    );
    94	    return () => subscription.remove();
    95	  }, []);
    96	
    97	  const afterPairing = (_next: AfterPairing) => {
    98	    setCompanionOpen(false);
    99	    setNeedsMac(false);
   100	    setPairingKey(key => key + 1);
   101	  };
   102	  const closePairing = () => {
   103	    setCompanionOpen(false);
   104	    setPairingKey(key => key + 1);
   105	  };
   106	
   107	  return (
   108	    <SafeAreaProvider>
   109	      {/* One queue for every call to the Mac from the shell. */}
   110	      <RequestGateProvider>
   111	        {/* Keeps the paired Mac's credential alive. */}
   112	        <KeepPairingFresh onPairAgain={() => setPairingKey(key => key + 1)} />
   113	        <StatusBar
   114	          barStyle={
   115	            Platform.OS === 'ios' && scheme === 'dark'
   116	              ? 'light-content'
   117	              : 'dark-content'
   118	          }
   119	        />
   120	        {needsMac ? (
   121	          <FindYourMacScreen
   122	            onDone={afterPairing}
   123	            canScan={canScan}
   124	            // One camera at a time: this one waits under an open sheet.
   125	            paused={companionOpen}
   126	            link={companionOpen ? null : pairLink}
   127	            onLinkUsed={() => setPairLink(null)}
   128	          />
   129	        ) : (
   130	          <ObjectsApp
   131	            style={styles.app}
   132	            pairingKey={pairingKey}
   133	            onPair={() => setCompanionOpen(true)}
   134	          />
   135	        )}
   136	        {companionOpen ? (
   137	          <Modal presentationStyle="fullScreen" onRequestClose={closePairing}>
   138	            <FindYourMacScreen
   139	              onBack={closePairing}
   140	              onDone={afterPairing}
   141	              canScan={canScan}
   142	              resume
   143	              link={pairLink}
   144	              onLinkUsed={() => setPairLink(null)}
   145	            />
   146	          </Modal>
   147	        ) : null}
   148	        <ScanSupport onChange={setCanScan} />
   149	      </RequestGateProvider>
   150	    </SafeAreaProvider>
   151	  );
   152	}

---CAPTURE START STATUS DETAIL---
                 "activeRunID": Self.owner.activeRunID as Any? ?? NSNull()])
    }
    @objc func drafts(_ resolve: RCTPromiseResolveBlock, reject: RCTPromiseRejectBlock) {
        do { resolve(try Self.owner.store.list(activeRunID: Self.owner.activeRunID).map { try $0.dictionary() }) }
        catch { reject("storage-error", error.localizedDescription, error) }
    }
    @objc func start(_ options: NSDictionary, resolve: @escaping RCTPromiseResolveBlock,
                     reject: @escaping RCTPromiseRejectBlock) {
        guard ObjectCaptureSession.isSupported && PhotogrammetrySession.isSupported else {
            reject("unsupported", "Scanning is unavailable on this device. Choose a USDZ file instead.", nil); return
        }
        guard host == nil, let presenter = RCTPresentedViewController(), presenter.presentedViewController == nil else {
            reject("busy", "Close the current sheet first.", nil); return
        }
        do {
            let origin = try JSONDecoder().decode(NativeCaptureDestination.self,
                from: JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: options))
            let draft = try Self.owner.start(origin: origin)
            guard let engine = Self.owner.engine as? GuidedNativeCaptureEngine else {
                // Synchronous startup failure was retained by the owner.
                resolve(try Self.owner.store.read(draft.runID).dictionary()); return
            }
            completion = resolve; rejection = reject; presentedRunID = draft.runID
            let controller = UIHostingController(rootView: NativeCaptureHostView(owner: Self.owner, model: engine.model) { [weak self] add in self?.close(add: add) })
            controller.modalPresentationStyle = .fullScreen; controller.isModalInPresentation = true
            host = controller; presenter.present(controller, animated: true)
        } catch { reject("capture-error", error.localizedDescription, error) }
    }

+// MARK: - Hosting inside the React Native shell
+
+/// The React Native view that shows the SwiftUI app. `pairingKey` changes after pairing, so the app reloads;
+/// `onPair` asks the shell to show its pairing screen.
+@objc(STMObjectsAppView)
+public final class STMObjectsAppView: UIView {
+    @objc public var pairingKey: NSNumber = 0 { didSet { render() } }
+    @objc public var onPair: RCTDirectEventBlock? { didSet { store.onPair = { [weak self] in self?.onPair?([:]) } } }
+    private let store = ObjectsStore()
+    private var host: UIHostingController<ObjectsRootView>?
+
+    public override func didMoveToWindow() {
+        super.didMoveToWindow()
+        guard window != nil, host == nil else { return }
+        let host = UIHostingController(rootView: ObjectsRootView(store: store, pairingKey: pairingKey.intValue))
+        // A child of the nearest view controller, so navigation bars, sheets and dialogs work.
+        var responder: UIResponder? = self
+        while let next = responder?.next, !(next is UIViewController) { responder = next }
+        (responder?.next as? UIViewController)?.addChild(host)
+        host.view.frame = bounds
+        host.view.autoresizingMask = [.flexibleWidth, .flexibleHeight]
+        host.view.backgroundColor = .clear
+        addSubview(host.view)
+        host.didMove(toParent: host.parent)
+        self.host = host
+    }
+    private func render() { host?.rootView = ObjectsRootView(store: store, pairingKey: pairingKey.intValue) }
+    public override func layoutSubviews() { super.layoutSubviews(); host?.view.frame = bounds }
+}

struct MacError: Error { let code: String; let message: String; var needsPairing: Bool { false }; static let unreachable = MacError(code: \"unreachable\", message: \"unreachable\") }
struct Object { var id: String }
@MainActor final class FakeMac {
 var created: [Object] = []
 var files: Set<String> = [\"photo.jpg\"]
 var starts = 0
 func create(_ instructions: String) async throws -> Object { let o = Object(id: UUID().uuidString); created.append(o); return o }
 func sendChosen(_ id: String, _ photos: [String]) async throws {
  guard photos.allSatisfy({ files.contains("'$0) }) else { throw MacError(code: "unreadable-file", message: "A file from this scan couldn'"'t be read.\") }
  // Same successful-send cleanup as ObjectsBridge.mm:348-350.
  for photo in photos { files.remove(photo) }
 }
 func start(_ id: String) async throws -> Object { starts += 1; if starts == 1 { throw MacError.unreachable }; return Object(id:id) }
}
@MainActor final class FakeStore { let mac = FakeMac(); func onPair() {} }
@MainActor final class FormProbe {

 var photos = [\"photo.jpg\"]
 var working: String?; var problem: String?; var askedForDescription = false; var describing = false
 var described: Bool { true }
 var completions = 0
 func dismiss() {}
 func started(_ o: Object) { completions += 1 }

 func attempt() async { await make() }

   490	    private func addPhotos() async {
   491	        describing = false
   492	        await run("photos") { photos += try await store.mac.choosePhotos(limit: max(1, 12 - photos.count)) }
   493	    }
   494	    private func make() async {
   495	        guard described else { askedForDescription = true; describing = true; return }
   496	        describing = false
   497	        await run("make") {
   498	            let object = try await store.mac.create(instructions)
   499	            try await store.mac.sendChosen(object.id, photos)
   500	            started(try await store.mac.start(object.id))
   501	        }
   502	    }
   503	    private func scan() async {
   504	        await run("scan") {
   505	            let object = try await store.mac.create(instructions)
   506	            if let run = try await store.mac.scan(object.id) { started(try await store.mac.sendScan(object.id, run: run)) }
   507	            else { started(object) }
   508	        }
   509	    }

---RULE SUPPORT---
     3	Build an open-source iPhone app that scans a real object with LiDAR and cleans up the scan's defects. David, 2026-10-31: "this app is so you can scan an object USING LIDAR on iphone. and then smooth out defects and such. but really anything after that is already built and downloaded by 10k people a month and it's my bambu mcp. later we may add mcp 3d printer. actually, may as well just have that be an option from the get go."
     4	
     5	The app covers capture (LiDAR Object Capture plus close-up stills) and cleanup (`tools/scan-repair`, `tools/scan-correct`, `tools/capture-mesh-convert`, run by the companion). The clean mesh then goes to [bambu-printer-mcp](https://github.com/DMontgomery40/bambu-printer-mcp) or [mcp-3D-printer-server](https://github.com/DMontgomery40/mcp-3D-printer-server) (`/Users/davidmontgomery/mcp-3D-printer-server` on the Mac). The handoff is built: the companion relays it through `services/printer-handoff` (#124), and the phone's Send to printer flow answers the printer's questions (#132). The consumer journey and Mac app design are in [`docs/design/journey.md`](docs/design/journey.md).
77:Devices without LiDAR run the app fully but can't scan. They say scanning needs a LiDAR device. There is no photo-only substitute. iPad and the foldable iPhone get real layouts: folded is the iPhone layout, open is two panes, and iPad is sidebar plus detail.

codex
TypeScript, lint, 52 Jest tests, and 6 Swift model tests pass. However, capture recovery and failed-start retries regress, selected attachments can be lost, and the new icon blocks distribution uploads.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Resume finished capture drafts when reopening an object — /Users/davidmontgomery/stm-wt/KAN-206/apps/mobile/ios/ScanToMake/Objects/ObjectsApp.swift:553-553
  If the app closes after reconstruction finishes but before the first upload succeeds, reopening the object now only reloads its server record. `finishedScan` is called only by Finish Sending, but that action is unavailable when the Mac has received neither scan nor photos. The retained capture therefore cannot be recovered without scanning again. Restore the previous automatic draft lookup and resumable upload when opening a new object.

- [P2] Reuse the created object when retrying Make It — /Users/davidmontgomery/stm-wt/KAN-206/apps/mobile/ios/ScanToMake/Objects/ObjectsApp.swift:498-500
  If photo upload succeeds but `/start` fails, `sendChosen` has already deleted the cached photos. The form remains open, and another Make It tap creates a different object before attempting to upload those deleted files, producing an unreadable-file error and leaving duplicate objects. Retain the created object ID and resume its remaining steps instead of creating another object on every attempt.

- [P2] Send selected reference photos on the scan path — /Users/davidmontgomery/stm-wt/KAN-206/apps/mobile/ios/ScanToMake/Objects/ObjectsApp.swift:503-507
  When someone adds reference photos and then chooses Scan It Instead, this path never sends the selected `photos`. `sendScan` uploads only the capture's own images and immediately starts processing, silently dropping the attachments shown in the form. Send the chosen photos to the same object before starting processing, or explicitly prevent this unsupported combination.

- [P2] Preserve split navigation on iPad and unfolded Duo — /Users/davidmontgomery/stm-wt/KAN-206/apps/mobile/ios/ScanToMake/Objects/ObjectsApp.swift:227-228
  On iPad and unfolded Duo, the unconditional `NavigationStack` replaces the existing wide-screen list/detail layout with phone-style navigation, hiding the list whenever an object opens. This regresses the side-by-side layout required by the consumer journey referenced in [AGENTS.md:5](../../../AGENTS.md#L5). Restore adaptive split navigation for regular-width windows.

- [P2] Surface deletion conflicts in the object list — /Users/davidmontgomery/stm-wt/KAN-206/apps/mobile/ios/ScanToMake/Objects/ObjectsApp.swift:322-322
  Deleting an object during touch-up or printing returns HTTP 409, which the delete handler stores as a `conflict` error. This condition excludes that error and instead displays Connected to your Mac, so the failed deletion has no visible explanation. Display deletion errors separately, or disable deletion while the server forbids it.

- [P2] Clear polling errors after a successful reload — /Users/davidmontgomery/stm-wt/KAN-206/apps/mobile/ios/ScanToMake/Objects/ObjectsApp.swift:563-566
  If a polling request fails during touch-up or printing, `problem` receives the connection error. Subsequent successful polls update the object but never clear that error, leaving the page reporting that the Mac is unreachable even after processing finishes successfully. Clear recovered fetch errors on success, keeping them separate from action errors if necessary.

- [P2] Export the app icon without an alpha channel — /Users/davidmontgomery/stm-wt/KAN-206/apps/mobile/scripts/logo/draw.swift:6-7
  For App Store or TestFlight distribution, this generates an invalid app icon: the committed 1024-pixel PNG has an alpha channel, which also remains in the compiled icons. Apple rejects app icons containing alpha even when every pixel is opaque. Export the app-icon variant without alpha while preserving transparency for the rounded logo assets.
